“Approacheth now ye moment of payement. Ye ravens are flying. Ye bats winge against ye skye. There are skulls in ye starres. Ye soul & ye bodie are promised and shall bee delivered uppe. Not to ye dust agayne nor ye elements from which springe lyfe﻿—”
The candle flickered slightly. I tried to scream, but my mouth gaped to a soundless yammering. I tried to flee, but I stood frozen, unable even to close my eyes.
“﻿—﻿—﻿Ye abysse yawns & ye debt is to paye. Ye light fayles, ye shadows gather. There is no god but evil; no lite but darkness; no hope but doom﻿—”
A hollow groan resounded through the room. *It seemed to come from the robe-covered thing on the table!* That robe twitched fitfully.
“Oh winges in ye black darke!”

===Chinese===

“‘奥甘萨·纳·舒巴（Augantha na shuba）……’

在那蛮荒可怖的音节吐露而出的第一刹那，一只冰冷刺骨的恐惧魔爪狠狠扼住了我的咽喉。我惶恐绝望的双目猛然扫向长桌四周的烛台，果不其然，又一支蜡烛在无声中噗然熄灭。然而屋内并无半点穿堂阴风能吹动厚重漆黑的挂毯。康拉德的声线开始剧烈颤抖；他扬手按住自己的咽喉，呼吸骤然陷入短暂的窒息干呕。而那位东方人的双眸却自始至终未曾有一丝波澜。

“‘……于人子众生之间，诡谲幽影永恒潜行游弋。世人唯见尖爪撕裂之足印，未尝窥见踏足之魔躯。于生灵万民之魂魄上，垂天黑翼铺展遮蔽。冥界黑主虽得凡人呼以撒旦（Sathanas）、别西卜（Beelzebub）、阿波伦（Apolleon）、阿里曼（Ahriman）乃至玛利克·图斯（Malik Tous）诸般尊名，然其至尊本尊唯有一位……’

---

无边无际的恐怖迷雾彻底将我吞没。我浑浑噩噩地感知到康拉德的声音仍在单调机械地回荡嗡鸣，一时是英语，一时又是那可怕异邦的污秽魔语，其间所蕴含的可怖深意我甚至不敢起念去揣度分毫。在这股扼住心脏的冰冷绝望中，我眼睁睁看着烛火一支接一支接连熄灭。随着阴影在四周如墨汁般愈发浓稠深重，我心中的恐惧亦呈山崩地裂之势疯狂递增。我无法言语，我动弹不得；我那暴突欲裂的眼球带着濒死的剧痛狂乱凝视着仅存的烛苗。伫立在那阴森长桌之首的沉默东方人，同样成为了我极度恐惧的根源。他自始至终不发一言、未移半步，但在那微垂的眼睑之下，双眸中却喷涌着魔鬼般的狂喜与得逞之光；我深知在他那高深莫测的面具之下，正泛滥着残忍至极的狞恶自得——然而究竟是为何……究竟为何？

但我心底却比任何时刻都更加无比确凿地知晓——一旦最后一支蜡烛熄灭将整间屋宇拖入绝对黑暗的刹那，某种不可名状、令人作呕的滔天凶邪必将轰然降临！康拉德已然接近了咒文的终篇。他的声调在渐强的高潮中步步逼近极致。

“‘清算偿债之时刻赫然降临。渡鸦于天际翱翔。蝙蝠拍打苍穹之翼。星辰之中骷髅显形。魂魄与肉身既已立契许诺，必当如数全盘交托。绝不归于尘土抔土，亦不归于孕育万灵生机之诸般行质……’

那支残存的蜡烛微弱地摇曳晃动。我拼命想要尖叫求救，但张大的嘴唇间唯能发出无声的嘶鸣。我试图拔足狂奔，但整个人却如冻结的花岗岩般僵立原地，甚至连合上眼皮亦无法办到。

“‘……深渊赫然巨口大张，夙债立时结算交割。光明消歇，阴翳重聚。世间除恶别无神祇；除暗别无华光；除灭顶天谴别无指望……’

一声空洞凄厉的惨哼在整间书斋中轰然回荡。那声响分明是自长桌上那团被丝袍覆盖的尸骸喉间发出！紧接着，那袭丝袍开始抽搐般剧烈颤动！

“‘呜呼，沉沉至暗中的垂天之翼！’”

===Original===

I started violently; a faint swish sounded in the gathering shadows. The stir of the dark hangings? It sounded like the rustle of gigantic wings.
“Oh redde eyes in ye shadows! What is promised, what is writ in bloode is fulfilled! Ye lite is gulfed in blackness! Ya﻿—Koth!”
The last candle went out suddenly and a ghastly unhuman cry that came not from my lips or from Conrad’s burst unbearably forth. Horror swept over me like a black icy wave; in the blind dark I heard myself screaming terribly. Then with a swirl and a great rush of wind something swept the room, flinging the hangings aloft and dashing chairs and tables crashing to the floor. For an instant an intolerable odor burned our nostrils, a low hideous tittering mocked us in the blackness; then silence fell like a shroud.
Somehow, Conrad found a candle and lighted it. The faint glow showed us the room in fearful disarray﻿—showed us each other’s ghastly faces﻿—and showed us the black ebony table﻿—empty! The doors and windows were locked as they had been, but the oriental was gone﻿—and so was the corpse of John Grimlan.
Shrieking like damned men we broke down the door and fled frenziedly down the well-like staircase where the darkness seemed to clutch at us with clammy black fingers. As we tumbled down into the lower hallway, a lurid glow cut the darkness and the scent of burning wood filled our nostrils.
---
The outer doorway held momentarily against our frantic assault, then gave way and we hurtled into the outer starlight. Behind us the flames leaped up with a crackling roar as we fled down the hill. Conrad, glancing over his shoulder, halted suddenly, wheeled and flung up his arms like a madman, and screamed, “Soul and body he sold to Malik Tous, who is Satan, two hundred and fifty years ago! This was the night of payment﻿—and my God﻿—look! *Look!* The Fiend has claimed his own!”
I looked, frozen with horror. Flames had enveloped the whole house with appalling swiftness, and now the great mass was etched against the shadowed sky, a crimson inferno. And above the holocaust hovered a gigantic black shadow like a monstrous bat, and from its dark clutch dangled a small white thing, like the body of a man, dangling limply. Then, even as we cried out in horror, it was gone and our dazed gaze met only the shuddering walls and blazing roof which crumpled into the flames with an earthshaking roar.

===Chinese===

我猛地浑身剧烈一震；在愈发深沉的阴影中传来一声极微弱的破空呼啸。莫非是厚重挂毯被风拂动的声响？那听上去分明宛如遮天蔽日的庞然巨翼在沉重扇动！

“‘呜呼，重重阴影深处猩红的魔眸！既已立下盟约，既已用鲜血写就，万物皆已成就！光明尽归沉沦，没入无底长夜！呀——科斯（Ya﻿—Koth）！’

最后一支蜡烛骤然熄灭，一声绝非出自我和康拉德唇舌的凄厉非人惨嚎，撕心裂肺、无可阻挡地轰然炸响！恐怖如同一股漆黑冰冷的滔天巨浪将我瞬间没顶吞噬；在伸手不见五指的盲目黑暗中，我听见自己正在发出绝望至极的尖声惨叫。紧接着，伴随着一阵狂暴倒卷的阴风呼啸，某种庞然大物在房间中狂暴横扫而过，将重重挂毯猛然卷上半空，将桌椅柜台轰然撞碎在地面上。某一瞬，一股无法忍受的刺鼻恶臭狠狠灼痛了我们的鼻腔，一声低沉阴鸷的嗤嗤怪笑在漆黑中对我们极尽嘲弄；旋即，死寂如尸布般沉沉降临。

不知怎的，康拉德在混乱中摸出了一根蜡烛并将其点燃。昏黄惨淡的微光映照出一片狼藉破败的房间——映照出我们彼此惨白骇异的人形面孔——更是映照出那张巨大的黑檀木长桌——此刻已然空空如也！门窗依旧严丝合缝地紧紧锁闭，然而那位神秘的东方人却已鸿飞冥冥——而约翰·格里姆兰的遗蜕尸骸，亦同样消失得无影无踪！

我们发出宛如坠入阿鼻地狱受刑恶鬼般的狂叫，猛然撞开房门，发疯般沿着宛如深井般的楼梯狂奔而下，阴暗中仿佛有一只只黏腻冰冷的漆黑鬼手正拼命拉扯着我们的衣角。当我们跌跌撞撞滚落到底层门厅时，一抹猩红耀眼的火光撕裂了黑暗，燃烧木柴的刺鼻焦味瞬间充斥了鼻腔。

---

宅邸的大门在我们狂乱的撞击下稍稍阻滞了片刻，随即被轰然撞开，我们二人如炮弹般滚入了外头清冷的星光之下。在我们身后，熊熊烈焰伴随着噼啪炸响的轰鸣冲天而起，我们连滚带爬地向着山下狂奔逃命。康拉德猛然扭头回望，脚步骤然顿住，霍然转身，像个疯子般狂乱挥舞着双臂，发出撕心裂肺的狂叫：“两百五十年前（250年前），他将灵魂与肉身尽数卖给了玛利克·图斯——亦即撒旦本尊！今夜便是结算兑现之期——我的上帝啊——快看！快看！恶魔前来收缴他的战利品了！”

我骇然凝眸望去，整个人彻底冻结在极度的恐怖之中。大火以令人匪夷所思的狂暴神速瞬间吞没了整栋大宅，此刻那座庞然巨物在夜空的映衬下，化作了一座熊熊燃烧的猩红炼狱。而在那片冲天火海之上，赫然盘旋着一道宛如巨型蝠魔般的庞大黑影，在其幽暗的魔爪之下，正无力地晃荡悬挂着一具娇小惨白之物，那分明是一具软绵绵垂落的人类身躯。紧接着，就在我们失声惊呼的电光石火间，那魔影瞬间湮灭不见，我们目瞪口呆的视线中所能捕捉到的，唯有在山崩地裂的惊天轰鸣中战栗坍塌、彻底没入滔天火海中的焦黑残垣与烈焰倾覆的屋顶。

## Gods of the North / 北方群神
===Original===

The clangor of the swords had died away, the shouting of the slaughter was hushed; silence lay on the red-stained snow. The pale bleak sun that glittered so blindingly from the ice-fields and the snow-covered plains struck sheens of silver from rent corselet and broken blade, where the dead lay in heaps. The nerveless hand yet gripped the broken hilt: helmeted heads, back-drawn in the death throes, tilted red beards and golden beards grimly upward, as if in last invocation to Ymir the frost-giant.
Across the red drifts and mail-clad forms, two figures approached one another. In that utter desolation only they moved. The frosty sky was over them, the white illimitable plain around them, the dead men at their feet. Slowly through the corpses they came, as ghosts might come to a tryst through the shambles of a world.
Their shields were gone, their corselets dinted. Blood smeared their mail; their swords were red. Their horned helmets showed the marks of fierce strokes.
One spoke, he whose locks and beard were red as the blood on the sunlit snow.
“Man of the raven locks,” said he, “tell me your name, so that my brothers in Vanaheim may know who was the last of Wulfhere’s band to fall before the sword of Heimdul.”
“This is my answer,” replied the black-haired warrior: “Not in Vanaheim, but in Valhalla will you tell your brothers the name of Amra of Akbitana.”
Heimdul roared and sprang, and his sword swung in a mighty arc. Amra staggered and his vision was filled with red sparks as the blade shivered into bits of blue fire on his helmet. But as he reeled he thrust with all the power of his great shoulders. The sharp point drove through brass scales and bones and heart, and the red-haired warrior died at Amra’s feet.
Amra stood swaying, trailing his sword, a sudden sick weariness assailing him. The glare of the sun on the snow cut his eyes like a knife and the sky seemed shrunken and strangely far. He turned away from the trampled expanse where yellow-bearded warriors lay locked with red-haired slayers in the embrace of death. A few steps he took, and the glare of the snow fields was suddenly dimmed. A rushing wave of blindness engulfed him, and he sank down into the snow, supporting himself on one mailed arm, seeking to shake the blindness out of his eyes as a lion might shake his mane.
A silvery laugh cut through his dizziness, and his sight cleared slowly.

===Chinese===

长剑铿锵之鸣已然归于沉寂，血腥杀戮的嘶吼亦已平息；皑皑血雪之上，万籁俱寂。苍白凄冷的寒日自冰原与覆雪旷野上折射出炫目欲盲的寒芒，照耀在堆积如山的尸骸丛中，自破裂的护胸铁甲与折断的残刃上激起阵阵银色冷光。僵死无力的手掌依然死死攥着折断的剑柄；覆着战盔的头颅在濒死痉挛中后仰，赤髯与金须狰狞地直刺苍穹，宛如在向霜巨人尤弥尔（Ymir）作最后的祈告。

越过殷红的积雪与横陈的铠甲之躯，两道身影相对而行。在这片荒凉绝域之中，唯有他们尚在移动。头顶是冻结的苍穹，周遭是无垠的茫茫雪原，脚下是横七竖八的死者。他们踏着枕藉的尸骸缓缓行来，恰似幽魂赴约，穿越满目疮痍的修罗血海。

他们的盾牌已然碎裂无存，护甲凹陷残破。鲜血浸透了锁子甲；长剑一片猩红。牛角头盔上布满了狂暴劈砍留下的斑驳创痕。

其中一人开口，其髭须长发赤红如日光照耀下的血雪。

“黑发之人，”他说，“报上你的名号，好让我瓦纳海姆（Vanaheim）的同胞得知，伍尔夫赫尔（Wulfhere）麾下最后一名倒在海姆杜尔（Heimdul）剑下的勇士究竟是谁。”

“这便是我的答复，”黑发战士森然回道，“不是在瓦纳海姆，而是在英灵殿瓦尔哈拉（Valhalla），你自当向你的兄弟们吐露阿克比塔纳的阿姆拉（Amra of Akbitana）之名。”

海姆杜尔咆哮着飞身纵起，长剑在半空中划出一道巨大的凶暴圆弧。重剑斩在头盔上炸开万道冰蓝火星，震得阿姆拉眼前金星狂舞、踉跄倒退。然而就在身形摇晃的刹那，他挟着双肩狂暴的巨力悍然直刺。森寒的剑尖势如破竹，贯穿了黄铜鳞甲、胸骨与心脏，赤髯战士轰然倒毙在阿姆拉脚下。

阿姆拉拖着染血的长剑，摇摇晃晃地伫立当场，一股作呕般的极度倦意排山倒海般袭来。雪地上刺目的雪盲寒光如刀刃般剜割着他的眼球，苍穹仿佛骤然收缩、退向莫名深邃的远方。他转身背对这片践踏得泥泞不堪的战场——金须战士与赤发杀手在死亡的铁臂拥抱中纠缠长眠。他踉跄迈出数步，雪原的刺目强光骤然黯淡。一股眩目的漆黑盲潮将他彻底吞没，他单臂撑在雪中仆倒下去，像雄狮甩动鬃毛般极力晃动头颅，试图驱散眼前的盲黑。

一声银铃般的轻笑骤然穿透了他的眩晕，他的视野徐徐恢复清明。

===Original===

There was a strangeness about all the landscape that he could not place or define﻿—an unfamiliar tinge to earth and sky. But he did not think long of this. Before him, swaying like a sapling in the wind, stood a woman. Her body was like ivory, and save for a veil of gossamer, she was naked as the day. Her slender bare feet were whiter than the snow they spurned. She laughed, and her laughter was sweeter than the rippling of silvery fountains, and poisonous with cruel mockery.
“Who are you?” demanded the warrior.
“What matter?” Her voice was more musical than a silver-stringed harp, but it was edged with cruelty.
“Call up your men,” he growled, grasping his sword. “Though my strength fail me, yet they shall not take me alive. I see that you are of the Vanir.”
“Have I said so?”
He looked again at her unruly locks, which he had thought to be red. Now he saw that they were neither red nor yellow, but a glorious compound of both colors. He gazed spellbound. Her hair was like elfin-gold, striking which, the sun dazzled him. Her eyes were neither wholly blue nor wholly grey, but of shifting colors and dancing lights and clouds of colors he could not recognize. Her full red lips smiled, and from her slim feet to the blinding crown of her billowy hair, her ivory body was as perfect as the dream of a god. Amra’s pulse hammered in his temples.
“I can not tell,” said he, “whether you are of Vanaheim and mine enemy, or of Asgard and my friend. Far have I wandered, from Zingara to the Sea of Vilayet, in Stygia and Kush, and the country of the Hyrkanians; but a woman like you I have never seen. Your locks blind me with their brightness. Not even among the fairest daughters of the Aesir have I seen such hair, by Ymir!”
“Who are you to swear by Ymir?” she mocked. “What know you of the gods of ice and snow, you who have come up from the south to adventure among strangers?”
“By the dark gods of my own race!” he cried in anger. “Have I been backward in the swordplay, stranger or no? This day I have seen four score warriors fall, and I alone survive the field where Mulfhere’s reavers met the men of Bragi. Tell me, woman, have you caught the flash of mail across the snow-plains, or seen armed men moving upon the ice?”
“I have seen the hoarfrost glittering in the sun,” she answered. “I have heard the wind whispering across the everlasting snows.”
He shook his head.

===Chinese===

整片荒原上笼罩着一种他无法言说、难以名状的诡谲——大地与苍穹皆泛着一抹陌生的异样色泽。但他无暇深思。在他身前，一具宛如风中幼树般款款摇曳的窈窕身影俏然而立。她的躯体皎洁如象牙，除了一抹轻若游丝的薄纱外，寸缕不着，如初生之日般赤裸无瑕。她纤巧白皙的玉足比脚下践踏的寒雪还要胜雪三分。她轻声巧笑，那笑声比银泉漱玉更加甘洌悦耳，却淬满了残忍至极的戏谑毒刺。

“你究竟是谁？”战士沉声喝问。

“何足挂齿？”她的嗓音比银弦竖琴更动人心魄，却锋芒毕露、尽是残忍。

“叫出你的伏兵吧，”他握紧长剑低吼道，“纵使我力竭难支，他们亦休想生擒于我。我瞧得出你是华纳神族（Vanir）之人。”

“我曾这般说过吗？”

他又凝眸望向她那一头蓬松不羁的长发，方才他还以为那是赤红色的。此刻他方才发觉，那发丝非赤非金，而是将两种光华绚烂融汇的奇绝造化。他看得目瞪口呆。她的长发宛如妖精点化的秘金，日光折射其上，炫得他双目发晕。她的美眸非纯蓝亦非全灰，而是变幻莫测、光华流转，泛着他平生未见的斑斓霞彩。她饱满殷红的双唇噙着浅笑，从那双纤巧玉足直至如波涛般翻滚堆叠的灿金青丝，象牙般的胴体完美得恰似神祇的幻梦。阿姆拉太阳穴处的青筋狂乱搏动。

“我分辨不出，”他说，“你究竟来自瓦纳海姆是我的死敌，还是来自阿斯加德（Asgard）是我的同盟。我曾浪迹天涯，从辛加拉（Zingara）漂泊至维拉耶特海（Sea of Vilayet），踏足过斯泰吉亚（Stygia）、库什（Kush）以及希尔卡尼亚人（Hyrkanians）的疆土；但我平生从未见过如你一般的女子。你的发丝耀得我双目失明。以尤弥尔之名起誓，哪怕阿萨神族（Aesir）最绝色的神女，亦未曾拥有这般青丝！”

“你算什么东西，也配以尤弥尔起誓？”她讥嘲道，“你这从南方远道而来在异邦浪迹的游子，对冰雪之神又究竟懂得几分？”

“凭我家乡故土的幽暗诸神起誓！”他勃然大怒，“异邦人又何妨，我在剑术杀伐上可曾落后半步？就在今日，我亲眼目睹八十名勇士阵亡，而在这片穆尔夫赫尔（Mulfhere）的劫掠者遭遇布拉吉（Bragi）战团的修罗场上，唯有我一人浴血生还。告诉我，女人，你可曾在这雪原上瞥见铁甲寒光，可曾望见冰原上有披甲战士行进？”

“我唯见寒霜在日光下泛着凛冽微光，”她答道，“我唯闻寒风在永恒冻土上凄冷低语。”

他摇了摇头。

===Original===

“Niord should have come up with us before the battle joined. I fear he and his warriors have been ambushed. Wulfhere lies dead with all his weapon-men.
“I had thought there was no village within many leagues of this spot, for the war carried us far, but you can have come no great distance over these snows, naked as you are. Lead me to your tribe, if you are of Asgard, for I am faint with the weariness of strife.”
“My dwelling place is further than you can walk, Amra of Akbitana!” she laughed. Spreading wide her arms she swayed before him, her golden head lolling wantonly, her scintillant eyes shadowed beneath long silken lashes. “Am I not beautiful, man?”
“Like Dawn running naked on the snows,” he muttered, his eyes burning like those of a wolf.
“Then why do you not rise and follow me? Who is the strong warrior who falls down before me?” she chanted in maddening mockery. “Lie down and die in the snow with the other fools, Amra of the black hair. You can not follow where I would lead.”
With an oath the man heaved himself upon his feet, his blue eyes blazing his dark scarred face convulsed. Rage shook his soul, but desire for the taunting figure before him hammered at his temples and drove his wild blood riotously through his veins. Passion fierce as physical agony flooded his whole being so that earth and sky swam red to his dizzy gaze, and weariness and faintness were swept from him in madness.
He spoke no word as he drove at her fingers hooked like talons. With a shriek of laughter she leaped back and ran, laughing at him over her white shoulder. With a low growl Amra followed. He had forgotten the fight, forgotten the mailed warriors who lay in their blood, forgotten Niord’s belated reavers. He had thought only for the slender white shape which seemed to float rather than run before him.
Out across the white blinding plain she led him. The trampled red field fell out of sight behind him, but still Amra kept on with the silent tenacity of his race. His mailed feet broke through the frozen crust; he sank deep in the drifts and forged through them by sheer strength. But the girl danced across the snow as light as a feather floating across a pool; her naked feet scarcely left their imprint on the hoarfrost.

===Chinese===

“交锋之前，尼奥尔德（Niord）本该率部与我们会合。我只恐他与麾下勇士已遭伏击。伍尔夫赫尔已与满阵带刀勇士尽数阵亡。

“这鏖战将我们引向极远荒域，我本以为方圆数里之内断无村落；而你这般赤身裸体，断不可能徒步穿越重重极寒雪原走得太远。若你来自阿斯加德，便带我前往你的部族吧，因这血战搏杀的疲惫已令我虚弱不堪。”

“我栖居之所，远非你这凡胎所能涉足，阿克比塔纳的阿姆拉！”她放肆娇笑。她张开双臂，在他面前款款摇曳，金发慵懒放浪地披散，璀璨的美眸在浓密如丝的长睫下顾盼生辉。“凡人，难道我不美吗？”

“美若拂晓赤裸奔行于皑皑白雪之上，”他喉头滚烫喃喃低语，双眸如饿狼般凶光炽盛。

“那你为何不起身随我而来？那倒伏在我脚下的强横战士又是何人？”她以令人发狂的轻蔑嘲弄吟唱道，“躺下吧，同那些愚夫蠢汉一道暴毙在雪地里吧，黑发的阿姆拉。我所引之路，你根本无力企及。”

伴随着一声狂暴的咒骂，汉子霍然翻身跃起，碧蓝的双眼喷涌着狂暴的怒火，黝黑刀疤纵横的面庞因狰狞而极度扭曲。暴怒震颤着他的灵魂，然而对眼前这具轻佻尤物的炽烈渴求，却在太阳穴狂乱轰鸣，驱使着他周身的狂暴热血在血管中歇斯底里地疯狂奔涌。烈如酷刑的原始欲焰吞没了他的全部神智，令天地在他晕眩的视野中化作一片血红，疲惫与虚弱瞬间在这股癫狂暴戾中荡然无存。

他未发一言，五指弯曲如钩，如恶虎扑食般朝她暴掠而去。伴随着一声刺耳狂放的尖笑，少女轻盈后跃，扭动白皙如玉的双肩回头对他极尽嘲弄，随即拔足飞奔。阿姆拉从喉底发出一声低沉的狂嚎，紧追不舍。他早已忘却了方才的血战，忘却了横卧血泊之中的披甲亡魂，忘却了迟迟未至的尼奥尔德猎队。他满心唯有眼前那道与其说是在奔跑、不如说是在雪地上御风飘行的纤巧雪白娇躯。

她引着他，踏向无边无际白茫茫的死寂雪原。泥泞践踏的血色战场被远远抛入脑后，但阿姆拉依然凭借着部族骨子里那股沉寂坚韧的蛮性死咬不放。他覆着铁甲的重靴踏碎坚硬的冻雪冰壳，深陷雪窝，唯凭一身蛮力强行涉雪破浪。然而那少女在雪原上翩然起舞，轻盈得恰似掠过池塘的水鸟羽毛；她赤裸的无瑕玉足，甚至未曾在严霜上留下半点践踏印痕。

===Original===

In spite of the fire in his veins, the cold bit through the warrior’s mail and furs; but the girl in her gossamer veil ran as lightly and as gaily as if she danced through the palms and rose gardens of Poitain.
Black curses drooled through the warrior’s parched lips. The great veins swelled and throbbed in his temples, and his teeth gnashed spasmodically.
“You can not escape me!” he roared. “Lead me into a trap and I’ll pile the heads of your kinsmen at your feet. Hide from me and I’ll tear apart the mountains to find you! I’ll follow you to hell and beyond hell!”
Her maddening laughter floated back to him, and foam flew from the warrior’s lips. Further and further into the wastes she led him, till he saw the wide plains give way to low hills, marching upward in broken ranges. Far to the north he caught a glimpse of towering mountains, blue with the distance, or white with the eternal snows. Above these mountains shone the flaring rays of the borealis. They spread fan-wise into the sky, frosty blades of cold flaming light, changing in color, growing and brightening.
Above him the skies glowed and crackled with strange lights and gleams. The snow shone weirdly, now frosty blue, now icy crimson, now cold silver. Through a shimmering icy realm of enchantment Amra plunged doggedly onward, in a crystaline maze where the only reality was the white body dancing across the glittering snow beyond his reach﻿—ever beyond his reach.
Yet he did not wonder at the necromantic strangeness of it all, not even when two gigantic figures rose up to bar his way. The scales of their mail were white with hoarfrost; their helmets and their axes were sheathed in ice. Snow sprinkled their locks; in their beards were spikes of icicles; their eyes were cold as the lights that streamed above them.
“Brothers!” cried the girl, dancing between them. “Look who follows! I have brought you a man for the feasting! Take his heart that we may lay it smoking on our father’s board!”
The giants answered with roars like the grinding of icebergs on a frozen shore, and heaved up their shining axes as the maddened Akbitanan hurled himself upon them. A frosty blade flashed before his eyes, blinding him with its brightness, and he gave back a terrible stroke that sheared through his foe’s thigh.

===Chinese===

尽管血管中如岩浆般炽烈翻滚，刺骨的严寒依然透过战士的重甲与兽皮狠狠咬噬肌骨；然而那仅披一抹轻纱的少女，却奔跑得那般轻灵欢愉，宛如正在波坦（Poitain）的棕榈林与玫瑰园中漫步起舞。

漆黑的恶毒咒骂自战士干裂的双唇间滚滚喷涌。太阳穴两侧粗大的青筋暴突狂跳，他的牙关剧烈挫动，咯咯作响。

“你休想逃出我的手心！”他如雷咆哮，“纵使你引我踏入陷阱，我也要将你族人的头颅堆成小山扔在你的脚下！纵使你遁地藏形，我也要将山峦撕裂将你揪出！上穷碧落下黄泉，我也誓要将你追捕到底！”

她那令人发狂的银铃娇笑在风中飘摇回荡，逼得战士嘴角激出串串白沫。她引着他一路深入蛮荒穷荒，直至广袤辽阔的平原逐渐让位于低矮连绵的丘陵，如犬牙交错般步步耸立。在极北的极目尽头，他隐约瞥见了高耸入云的巍峨雪山，因路途遥远而泛着苍茫的幽蓝，峰顶覆着亘古不化的万载玄冰。在那些雪山之巅，北极极光（borealis）正喷吐着变幻莫测的绚烂烈焰。极光如巨扇般横贯苍穹，化作一柄柄由冰冷焰光铸就的霜冻寒刃，色泽斑斓流转，愈发瑰丽璀璨。

头顶上方，天穹在诡异莫测的绚烂神芒中熊熊燃烧、噼啪作响。雪原折射出妖异的华光，时而泛着幽蓝寒霜，时而化作冰冷猩红，时而流转出凄冷银辉。穿行于这片宛如施了障眼巫术的冰晶幻境，阿姆拉宛如一头凶兽般顽强向前猛冲，在这片冰晶迷宫之中，世间唯一的真实，唯有前方雪原上那具翩翩起舞、永远近在咫尺却永远触不可及的雪白娇躯。

然而，对于这一切宛如通灵妖术般的怪诞景象，他竟浑然不觉，甚至当两尊如冰山般庞大巍峨的巨影赫然挡住去路之时，他亦未曾有半点迟疑。那两尊巨人体表的锁子甲鳞片上结满了厚重的白霜；他们的头盔与战斧尽皆封冻在坚冰之中。雪花散落在他们的浓密毛发间；长髯中垂挂着森森冰棱；那双眼眸冰冷刺骨，恰如头顶激荡翻卷的极光。

“哥哥们！”少女在两尊巨人之间翩然起舞，娇呼道，“快看谁追来了！我给你们带来了一个供宴飨大嚼的活人！快剖出他的心脏，好让我们将冒着腾腾热气的血心供奉在父亲的案头！”

巨人们发出了宛如冰山撞击冻土海岸般的轰鸣咆哮，高高扬起了雪亮的冰霜巨斧；与此同时，暴怒欲狂的阿克比塔纳汉子已然狂啸着合身扑上！一道森寒的斧刃在他眼前电闪而过，炫目的寒光刺得他双目发盲，而他旋即还以一记狂暴绝伦的返身重劈，剑刃摧枯拉朽般生生斩断了对手的大腿！

===Original===

With a groan the victim fell, and at the instant Amra was dashed into the snow, his left shoulder numb from the blow of the survivor, from which the warrior’s mail had barely saved his life. Amra saw the remaining giant looming above him like a colossus carved of ice, etched against the glowing sky. The axe fell, to sink through the snow and deep into the frozen earth as Amra hurled himself aside and leaped to his feet. The giant roared and wrenched the axe-head free, but even as he did so, Amra’s sword sang down. The giant’s knees bent and he sank slowly into the snow which turned crimson with the blood that gushed from his half-severed neck.
Amra wheeled, to see the girl standing a short distance away, staring in wide-eyed horror, all mockery gone from her face. He cried out fiercely and the blood-drops flew from his sword as his hand shook in the intensity of his passion.
“Call the rest of your brothers!” he roared. “Call the dogs! I’ll give their hearts to the wolves!”
With a cry of fright she turned and fled. She did not laugh now, nor mock him over her shoulder. She ran as for her life, and though he strained every nerve and thew, until his temples were like to burst and the snow swam red to his gaze, she drew away from him, dwindling in the witch-fire of the skies, until she was a figure no bigger than a child, then a dancing white flame on the snow, then a dim blur in the distance. But grinding his teeth until the blood started from his gums, he reeled on, and he saw the blur grow to a dancing white flame, and then she was running less than a hundred paces ahead of him, and slowly the space narrowed, foot by foot.
She was running with effort now, her golden locks blowing free; he heard the quick panting of her breath, and saw a flash of fear in the look she cast over her alabaster shoulder. The grim endurance of the warrior had served him well. The speed ebbed from her flashing white legs; she reeled in her gait. In his untamed soul flamed up the fires of hell she had fanned so well. With an inhuman roar he closed in on her, just as she wheeled with a haunting cry and flung out her arms to fend him off.
His sword fell into the snow as he crushed her to him. Her supple body bent backward as she fought with desperate frenzy in his iron arms.

===Chinese===

伴随着一声凄厉的闷哼，被斩中者轰然倒塌；与此同时，阿姆拉被幸存巨人的重击猛然轰翻在雪地之中，左肩彻底麻木失去知觉——若非身上的精钢锁甲护体，那一击早已取了他的性命。阿姆拉瞥见那残存的巨人如一座由寒冰雕凿而成的太古巨像般巍然耸立在他头顶，巨大的身躯在熊熊燃烧的绚烂极光映衬下凶相毕露。战斧轰然劈落，深深劈入积雪并狠扎进坚硬冻土之中；阿姆拉奋力侧滚翻身，弹跳跃起。巨人狂暴咆哮着欲将深深咬入泥土的斧刃拔出，然而尚未等他发力，阿姆拉的长剑已化作一道凄厉的龙吟呼啸斩落！巨人的双膝骤然一软，庞大的身躯缓缓沉入积雪之中；自被斩断大半的脖颈中狂喷而出的炽热鲜血，瞬间将周遭的茫茫冰原染得一片猩红。

阿姆拉猛然旋身，只见那少女正伫立在不远处，眼眸睁大，满脸惊骇欲绝，唇边戏谑的轻蔑嘲弄已然荡然无存。他发出如狂兽般的暴烈狂吼，伴随着他因极度狂暴的欲焰而剧烈战栗的手腕，长剑上的血珠在半空中四散飞溅。

“叫你剩下的哥哥们都滚出来！”他如雷鸣般咆哮，“把那些恶犬全叫来！我要把他们的心肝全剁碎喂狼！”

少女发出一声惊恐至极的尖叫，霍然转身仓皇狂逃。此时此刻她再也无法轻笑，亦不敢回头投来嘲讽的目光。她亡命奔逃；尽管战士调动了周身每一根筋骨与血脉狂追不舍，乃至太阳穴几乎要当场爆裂、积雪在他眼中化作一片血海，她依然逐渐拉开了距离，在漫天妖异极光的映衬下身形不断缩减，起初缩如孩童，继而化作雪原上一团狂乱跳跃的雪白火苗，最终沦为极目远方一抹模糊难辨的幽暗残影。然而阿姆拉死死咬碎钢牙，甚至任由鲜血自齿龈间渗出，依然踉跄着疯魔般紧追不舍；只见那抹残影再度扩大为跳动的白焰，转瞬间她已被逼近至不足百步之遥，而彼此间的距离，更在一尺一寸地无情收缩。

她此刻已是气喘吁吁、步履维艰，一头金发在风中狂乱飞舞；他听到了她急促紊乱的剧烈喘息，看到了她回首越过象牙般双肩望来时眼眸中掠过的那抹彻骨惊惶。战士那如磐石般残忍的坚毅耐力终究没有辜负他。她那双雪白修长的大腿渐渐失去了破风的神速；她的步履开始虚浮踉跄。在她亲手点燃的熊熊欲火烘烤下，战士未驯的狂暴灵魂中喷薄出了九幽地狱般的烈焰。伴随着一声非人的狂暴咆哮，阿姆拉如鹰隼扑食般合身扑上，正赶上她发出一声绝望凄厉的悲啼，霍然转身张开双臂试图阻挡他的扑击。

长剑坠落在雪地之中，他一把将她死死勒入怀中。在她于铁臂桎梏间展开的绝望狂乱反抗中，那柔若无骨的娇躯被向后生生扳折。

===Original===

Her golden hair blew about his face, blinding him with its sheen; the feel of her slender figure twisting in his mailed arms drove him to blinder madness. His strong fingers sank deep into her smooth flesh, and that flesh was cold as ice. It was as if he embraced not a woman of human flesh and blood, but a woman of flaming ice. She writhed her golden head aside, striving to avoid the savage kisses that bruised her red lips.
“You are cold as the snows,” he mumbled dazedly. “I will warm you with the fire in my own blood﻿—”
With a desperate wrench she twisted from his arms, leaving her single gossamer garment in his grasp. She sprang back and faced him, her golden locks in wild disarray, her white bosom heaving, her beautiful eyes blazing with terror. For an instant he stood frozen, awed by her terrible beauty as she posed naked against the snows.
And in that instant she flung her arms toward the lights that glowed in the skies above her and cried out in a voice that rang in Amra’s ears forever after:
“*Ymir! Oh, my father, save me!*”
Amra was leaping forward, arms spread to seize her, when with a crack like the breaking of an ice mountain, the whole skies leaped into icy fire. The girl’s ivory body was suddenly enveloped in a cold blue flame so blinding that the warrior threw up his hands to shield his eyes. A fleeting instant, skies and snowy hills were bathed in crackling white flames, blue darts of icy light, and frozen crimson fires. Then Amra staggered and cried out. The girl was gone. The glowing snow lay empty and bare; high above him the witch-lights flashed and played in a frosty sky gone mad and among the distant blue mountains there sounded a rolling thunder as of a gigantic war-chariot rushing behind steeds whose frantic hoofs struck lightning from the snows and echoes from the skies.
Then suddenly the borealis, the snowy hills and the blazing heavens reeled drunkenly to Amra’s sight; thousands of fireballs burst with showers of sparks, and the sky itself became a titanic wheel which rained stars as it spun. Under his feet the snowy hills heaved up like a wave, and the Akbitanan crumpled into the snows to lie motionless.
---
In a cold dark universe, whose sun was extinguished eons ago, Amra felt the movement of life, alien and unguessed.

===Chinese===

她的灿金青丝扑打在他的面庞上，炫目的光泽耀得他双目昏花；那具在锁甲臂弯中剧烈扭动挣扎的纤细娇躯，更逼得他彻底陷入无可救药的癫狂。他孔武有力的五指深深陷入她温润滑腻的凝脂皮肉之中，而那皮肉竟冰冷得宛如坚冰。那一刹那，他仿佛拥抱的根本不是一个由血肉铸就的凡胎女子，而是一个通体由炽烈坚冰雕琢而成的极寒魔女。她拼命扭动金发头颅避让，极力躲闪着他那将她娇艳红唇撕扯得一片青紫的狂暴吻咬。

“你冷得像这漫天寒雪，”他神智昏沉地喃喃低语，“我要用自己骨血中的烈焰将你彻底融化……”

少女猛然使出一股绝望的极力拧转，生生从他的怀抱中挣脱而出，唯将身上那件唯一的轻纱薄衣留在了他的掌心之中。她纵身向后暴退，与他怒目相对，满头金发凌乱如瀑，雪白的酥胸剧烈起伏，倾国倾城的美眸中喷涌着惊骇欲绝的恐惧。在这一刹那，面对着她赤身裸体傲立于皑皑白雪之上的惊世骇俗之美，阿姆拉竟被震慑得僵立当场。

而就在这一瞬之间，少女朝着头顶苍穹熊熊燃烧的漫天极光猛然扬起双臂，用一声在阿姆拉余生耳畔永恒回荡的凄厉尖啸破空长呼：

“尤弥尔（Ymir）！呜呼，我的父神，救我！”

阿姆拉正张开双臂纵身扑上欲将其重新生擒，然而伴随着一声宛如冰山崩裂般的惊天巨响，整片苍穹骤然腾起万丈冰蓝极火！少女象牙般的玉体瞬间被包裹在一团刺目欲盲的冰蓝烈焰之中，炫目得令战士下意识扬手遮挡双眸。在电光石火的转瞬之间，天穹与覆雪群山尽皆沐浴在噼啪作响的惨白烈焰、冰冷刺骨的幽蓝光矢以及冻结的猩红极光之中。紧接着，阿姆拉身形踉跄，失声惊呼。少女已然鸿飞冥冥。泛着妖光的雪原上空空如也，唯余无边死寂；高悬头顶的苍穹之上，妖异的磷火在癫狂暴走的寒夜太虚中疯狂狂舞，而在极目远方的苍茫蓝山之间，隐隐轰鸣起一阵宛如万马奔腾般的滚滚雷鸣，恰似一辆由神骏狂奔拉曳的巍峨战车正狂飙而过，狂乱的铁蹄在雪原上踏出万道雷霆闪电，在九天之上激起无尽回响。

骤然间，漫天极光、雪覆丘陵与燃烧的天幕在阿姆拉晕眩的眼前狂乱旋转倾覆；千万颗硕大的火球伴随着四散飞溅的星雨轰然炸裂，整座苍穹化作了一只疯狂旋转、喷洒漫天星辉的泰坦神轮。在他脚下，覆雪的山峦如滔天巨浪般剧烈起伏翻涌，这位阿克比塔纳汉子轰然瘫倒在雪原之中，一动不动地陷入死寂。

---

在一个恒星早已在亿万年前湮灭熄灭的冰冷黑暗宇宙深处，阿姆拉感知到了一股未知异样的生机在悄然律动。

===Original===

An earthquake had him in its grip and was shaking him to and fro, at the same time chafing his hands and feet until he yelled in pain and fury and groped for his sword.
“He’s coming to, Horsa,” grunted a voice. “Haste﻿—we must rub the frost out of his limbs, if he’s ever to wield sword again.”
“He won’t open his left hand,” growled another, his voice indicating muscular strain. “He’s clutching something﻿—”
Amra opened his eyes and stared into the bearded faces that bent over him. He was surrounded by tall golden-haired warriors in mail and furs.
“Amra! You live!”
“By Crom, Niord,” gasped he, “am I alive, or are we all dead and in Valhalla?”
“We live,” grunted the Aesir, busy over Amra’s half-frozen feet. “We had to fight our way through an ambush, else we had come up with you before the battle was joined. The corpses were scarce cold when we came upon the field. We did not find you among the dead, so we followed your spoor. In Ymir’s name, Amra, why did you wander off into the wastes of the north? We have followed your tracks in the snow for hours. Had a blizzard come up and hidden them, we had never found you, by Ymir!”
“Swear not so often by Ymir,” muttered a warrior, glancing at the distant mountains. “This is his land and the god bides among yonder mountains, the legends say.”
“I followed a woman,” Amra answered hazily. “We met Bragi’s men in the plains. I know not how long we fought. I alone lived. I was dizzy and faint. The land lay like a dream before me. Only now do all things seem natural and familiar. The woman came and taunted me. She was beautiful as a frozen flame from hell. When I looked at her I was as one mad, and forgot all else in the world. I followed her. Did you not find her tracks. Or the giants in icy mail I slew?”
Niord shook his head.
“We found only your tracks in the snow, Amra.”
“Then it may be I was mad,” said Amra dazedly. “Yet you yourself are no more real to me than was the golden haired witch who fled naked across the snows before me. Yet from my very hands she vanished in icy flame.”
“He is delirious,” whispered a warrior.
“Not so!” cried an older man, whose eyes were wild and weird. “It was Atali, the daughter of Ymir, the frost-giant! To fields of the dead she comes, and shows herself to the dying! Myself when a boy I saw her, when I lay half-slain on the bloody field of Wolraven.

===Chinese===

一场大地震死死攫住了他，将他剧烈地来回摇晃，同时疯狂揉搓着他的手足，痛得他暴怒狂吼着伸手去摸腰间的宝剑。

“霍萨（Horsa），他快苏醒了，”一个粗沉的嗓音嘟囔道，“动作快些——若想让他日后还能握得动剑，必须立刻把他四肢里的冻气全揉出来。”

“他的左手死活掰不开，”另一人沉声抱怨，话音中透着使出浑身解数的喘息，“他手心里死死攥着什么东西……”

阿姆拉缓缓睁开沉重的双眼，凝视着俯身在自己头顶的几张虬髯面孔。周遭尽是身披锁子甲与厚实兽皮、身材魁梧的金发战士。

“阿姆拉！你还活着！”

“凭克罗姆（Crom）起誓，尼奥尔德，”他上气不接下气地喘息道，“我还活着吗，还是说我们全军覆没、如今已身处英灵殿瓦尔哈拉了？”

“我们活得好好的，”这位阿萨神族（Aesir）大汉嘟囔着，手底下依然忙不迭地揉搓着阿姆拉半冻结的双脚，“我们被迫杀穿了一场埋伏，否则在开战之前便能赶来与你们合兵一处。等我们赶到战场时，尸首尚且温热。我们在死人堆里没寻见你的尸骸，便循着雪地上的足迹一路追踪而来。看在尤弥尔的份上，阿姆拉，你究竟因何缘故发疯般一路孤身闯入这片北方极寒绝域？我们循着雪地上的脚印足足追了几个时辰。以尤弥尔起誓，倘若中途刮起暴风雪将踪迹掩埋，我们至死也寻你不着！”

“休要动辄以尤弥尔之名发咒起誓，”一名战士警惕地望了一眼远方的群山，低声嘀咕，“相传这片冻土乃是他的领地，那位神祇本尊至今仍栖居在那座山峰深处。”

“我方才在追赶一个女人，”阿姆拉神思恍惚地答道，“我们在平原上与布拉吉的人马死战。我不知究竟搏杀了多久。唯有我一人浴血生还。彼时我头晕目眩、虚弱不堪。眼前的大地如梦似幻。唯有在此刻，周遭的一切方才显得真实寻常。那个女人现身嘲弄我。她美艳得恰似地狱深处一团冻结的烈火。每当我凝视着她，整个人便如痴如狂，将尘世间的一切抛诸脑后。我一路追赶着她。难道你们未曾寻见她的脚印？未曾瞧见那两个被我手刃、身披玄冰重甲的冰霜巨人？”

尼奥尔德神色凝重地摇了摇头。

“在雪地上，我们唯独寻见了你一人留下的足迹，阿姆拉。”

“如此说来，或许当真是我失心疯了，”阿姆拉失魂落魄地喃喃道，“然而，在我的感知之中，你们本人的真实，并不比方才在雪原上赤足奔逃的金发女巫真切半分。然而就在我的掌心之中，她化作一团极寒的冰焰，凭空湮灭了。”

“他神智错乱，正在说胡话呢，”一名战士耳语道。

“绝非胡话！”一位眼眸野性诡谲的老者霍然高呼，“那是阿塔莉（Atali），霜巨人尤弥尔之女！她向来只降临于死伤枕藉的杀戮修罗场，专向行将就木的垂死之人显现真容！当年我尚在年少之时，半死不活地躺在沃尔拉文（Wolraven）的尸山血海之中，便曾亲眼目睹过她的绝世芳容！

===Original===

I saw her walk among the dead in the snows, her naked body gleaming like ivory and her golden hair like a blinding flame in the moonlight. I lay and howled like a dying dog because I could not crawl after her. She lures men from stricken fields into the wastelands to be slain by her brothers, the ice-giants, who lay men’s red hearts smoking on Ymir’s board. Amra has seen Atali, the frost-giant’s daughter!”
“Bah!” grunted Horsa. “Old Gorm’s mind was turned in his youth by a sword cut on the head. Amra was delirious with the fury of battle. Look how his helmet is dinted. Any of those blows might have addled his brain. It was an hallucination he followed into the wastes. He is from the south; what does he know of Atali?”
“You speak truth, perhaps,” muttered Amra. “It was all strange and weird﻿—by Crom!”
He broke off, glaring at the object that still dangled from his clenched left fist; the others gaped silently at the veil he held up﻿—a wisp of gossamer that was never spun by human distaff.

===Chinese===

“我亲眼目睹她在雪地的尸骸丛中漫步，赤裸的胴体如象牙般泛着温润冷光，灿金的长发在皎洁月华下宛如一团令人目眩神迷的烈焰。我瘫倒在血泊中，像条濒死的野狗般凄厉哀嚎，只恨自己无力爬向她的裙下。她专门将重伤的凡人从修罗战场诱入极寒荒原，交由她的哥哥们——那群冰霜巨人残杀分尸，将凡人冒着热气的赤红心脏供奉在尤弥尔的案头。阿姆拉所见的，乃是不折不扣的阿塔莉，霜巨人之女！”

“呸！”霍萨粗声啐道，“老戈尔姆（Gorm）年轻时脑壳挨过一记重剑劈砍，脑子早就坏掉了。阿姆拉不过是被血战的狂怒冲昏了头脑。你们瞧瞧他的头盔凹陷成了何等模样。随便挨上那一记重劈，脑浆都得被震成一锅浆糊。他一路追入这片荒原的，不过是一场幻觉罢了。他本是个南方蛮子；对阿塔莉又能懂得几分？”

“或许你说得在理，”阿姆拉喃喃低语，“这一切当真太过怪诞离奇——以克罗姆（Crom）起誓！”

他的话语骤然僵死在半空，一双虎目死死盯住了依然紧紧攥在自己左拳之中的物什；周遭的众人顺着他的目光望去，尽皆目瞪口呆、陷入了死一般的沉寂——只见他高高扬起的掌心之中，赫然抓着一缕轻若游丝的蝉翼薄纱，那绝非尘世凡间任何纺车所能织就的神物。

## In the Forest of Villefère / 维勒夫尔森林中
===Original===

The sun had set. The great shadows came striding over the forest. In the weird twilight of a late summer day, I saw the path ahead glide on among the mighty trees and disappear. And I shuddered and glanced fearfully over my shoulder. Miles behind lay the nearest village﻿—miles ahead the next.
I looked to left and to right as I strode on, and anon I looked behind me. And anon I stopped short, grasping my rapier, as a breaking twig betokened the going of some small beast. Or was it a beast?
But the path led on and I followed, because, forsooth, I had naught else to do.
As I went I bethought me, “My own thoughts will rout me, if I be not aware. What is there in this forest, except perhaps the creatures that roam it, deer and the like? Tush, the foolish legends of those villagers!”
And so I went and the twilight faded into dusk. Stars began to blink and the leaves of the trees murmured in the faint breeze. And then I stopped short, my sword leaping to my hand, for just ahead, around a curve of the path, someone was singing. The words I could not distinguish, but the accent was strange, almost barbaric.
I stepped behind a great tree, and the cold sweat beaded my forehead.
Then the singer came in sight, a tall, thin man, vague in the twilight. I shrugged my shoulders. A man I did not fear. I sprang out, my point raised.
“Stand!”
He showed no surprise. “I prithee, handle thy blade with care, friend,” he said.
Somewhat ashamed, I lowered my sword.
“I am new to this forest,” I quoth, apologetically. “I heard talk of bandits. I crave pardon. Where lies the road to Villefère?”
“*Corbleu*, you’ve missed it,” he answered. “You should have branched off to the right some distance back. I am going there myself. If you may abide my company, I will direct you.”
I hesitated. Yet why should I hesitate?
“Why, certainly. My name is de Montour, of Normandy.”
“And I am Carolus le Loup.”
“No!” I started back.
He looked at me in astonishment.
“Pardon,” said I; “the name is strange. Does not *loup* mean wolf?”
“My family were always great hunters,” he answered. He did not offer his hand.
“You will pardon my staring,” said I as we walked down the path, “but I can hardly see your face in the dusk.”
I sensed that he was laughing, though he made no sound.
“It is little to look upon,” he answered.
I stepped closer and then leaped away, my hair bristling.
“A mask!” I exclaimed.

===Chinese===

夕阳已然沉落。巨大的重重树影如巨足般大步踏过森林。在夏末暮色苍茫的诡异薄暮中，我望见前方的小径在参天古木间蜿蜒穿行，旋即隐没在浓荫深处。我不禁浑身一颤，心怀戒惧地回头瞥了一眼身后。数英里之后是最近的村落，数英里之前方是下一处人烟。

我大步前行，目光警惕地扫视左右，并不时回头张望。突然间，枯枝折断的脆响预示着某种小型走兽悄然掠过，引得我骤然止步，右手紧紧扣住了细剑（rapier）剑柄。那当真是一头野兽吗？

然而小径在前方延伸，我唯有硬着头皮循路前行，因实情而言，我别无他途可走。

行进间，我暗自思忖：“若不自持警醒，心头的疑神疑鬼便先将我击溃了。这片林子里除了四处游荡的生灵——野鹿之流，还能有何物？咄，不过是乡民们荒诞不经的愚妄传说罢了！”

于是我继续赶路，薄暮渐渐沉入昏黑的夜色。星辰开始眨眼，树叶在微弱的晚风中窃窃私语。突然间，我猛地驻足，长剑已然电光石火般拔在手中——就在前方小径的拐弯处，竟有人正在引吭高歌。歌词我分辨不清，但那腔调古怪之极，近乎蛮荒粗粝。

我闪身避入一株巨树之后，额角沁出粒粒冷汗。

紧接着，歌者现身步入视线——一个身形高瘦、在夜色中轮廓模糊的男子。我耸了耸肩。肉体凡胎的活人我向来无所畏惧。我纵身跃出，剑尖直指来人。

“站住！”

他脸上全无半分惊愕。“万望兄台行行好，手底下剑刃留神，”他说道。

我心生几分赧颜，缓缓垂下剑锋。

“我初涉这片森林，”我略带歉意地说道，“此前曾听闻有匪盗出没。多有冒犯，恳请海涵。敢问前往维勒夫尔（Villefère）的道路该如何走？”

“哎呀（Corbleu），您可走岔了，”他答道，“您本该在方才后头的一处岔路向右拐。在下正巧也要前往该处。倘若阁下不嫌弃与我同行，我愿为您引路。”

我迟疑了片刻。然而我又何须迟疑？

“那敢情好。在下乃诺曼底的德·蒙图尔（de Montour, of Normandy）。”

“在下乃卡洛斯·勒·卢普（Carolus le Loup）。”

“不可能！”我骇然倒退一步。

他满脸错愕地凝视着我。

“抱歉，”我说道，“这名讳着实古怪。在法语中，loup岂非‘狼’之意？”

“我家族世代皆是赫赫有名的猎手，”他答道。他并未伸手行握手之礼。

“还望包涵我的打量，”我们并肩沿小道前行之际我开口道，“只是暮色昏沉，我近乎辨不清您的真容。”

我隐约察觉他在发笑，尽管他未发出半点声息。

“鄙人陋颜，本不足观，”他回道。

我凑近细瞧，随即吓得寒毛倒竖，猛然弹跳开去。

“一副面具！”我失声惊呼。

===Original===

“Why do you wear a mask, m’sieu?”
“It is a vow,” he explained. “In fleeing a pack of hounds I vowed that if I escaped I would wear a mask for a certain time.”
“Hounds, m’sieu?”
“Wolves,” he answered quickly; “I said wolves.”
We walked in silence for a while and then my companion said, “I am surprised that you walk these woods by night. Few people come these ways even in the day.”
“I am in haste to reach the border,” I answered. “A treaty has been signed with the English, and the Duke of Burgundy should know of it. The people at the village sought to dissuade me. They spoke of a﻿—wolf that was purported to roam these woods.”
“Here the path branches to Villefère,” said he, and I saw a narrow, crooked path that I had not seen when I passed it before. It led in amid the darkness of the trees. I shuddered.
“You wish to return to the village?”
“No!” I exclaimed. “No, no! Lead on.”
So narrow was the path that we walked single file, he leading. I looked well at him. He was taller, much taller than I, and thin, wiry. He was dressed in a costume that smacked of Spain. A long rapier swung at his hip. He walked with long easy strides, noiselessly.
Then he began to talk of travel and adventure. He spoke of many lands and seas he had seen and many strange things. So we talked and went farther and farther into the forest.
I presumed that he was French, and yet he had a very strange accent, that was neither French nor Spanish nor English, nor like any language I had ever heard. Some words he slurred strangely and some he could not pronounce at all.
“This path is not often used, is it?” I asked.
“Not by many,” he answered and laughed silently. I shuddered. It was very dark and the leaves whispered together among the branches.
“A fiend haunts this forest,” I said.
“So the peasants say,” he answered, “but I have roamed it oft and have never seen his face.”
Then he began to speak of strange creatures of darkness, and the moon rose and shadows glided among the trees. He looked up at the moon.
“Haste!” said he. “We must reach our destination before the moon reaches her zenith.”
We hurried along the trail.
“They say,” said I, “that a werewolf haunts these woodlands.”
“It might be,” said he, and we argued much upon the subject.

===Chinese===

“先生（m’sieu），您因何要戴着面具？”

“此乃誓约，”他解释道，“当年为摆脱一群恶犬围猎追杀，我曾立下重誓，倘若大难不死逃出生天，便须在特定时日内以面具遮面。”

“恶犬，先生？”

“狼群，”他迅疾改口道，“我方才说的是狼群。”

我们默默无言地行进了一段路程，随后我的同伴开口道：“阁下竟敢夤夜独穿这片老林，着实令我称奇。此地即便在白昼，亦极少有人涉足。”

“我急于赶赴边境，”我答道，“与英格兰人的和约业已缔结，勃艮第公爵理应尽早获悉。村落里的乡民曾竭力劝阻我前行。他们提及林中游弋着一头……传闻中的恶狼。”

“去往维勒夫尔的岔路便在此处，”他说道，顺着他的指引，我瞧见了一条先前路过时未曾察觉的狭窄曲折小径。那小道一路蜿蜒没入阴森漆黑的密林深处。我不禁打了个寒战。

“阁下莫非意欲折返村落？”

“绝不！”我脱口而出，“绝不，绝不！在前头带路吧。”

小径极为狭窄，我们唯有排成单列前行，由他在前方引路。我从背后仔细审视着他。他的身量比我高大得多，身形精瘦而筋骨强悍。他的装束隐隐透着几分西班牙风度。一柄长身细剑晃荡在他胯旁。他迈着轻快自如的大步，落地全无声息。

随后他开始闲谈起漫游与冒险。他谈及自己曾游历过的诸多异邦海疆，以及种种光怪陆离的见闻。我们就这般攀谈着，在森林深处愈走愈深。

我本推测他是法兰西人，但他说话却带着一种古怪绝伦的口音——既非法兰西语，亦非西班牙语或英格兰语，甚至不像我平生听闻过的任何言语体系。有些词句他含混吞吐得极为诡异，有些音节他甚至根本无法准确吐出。

“这条道平日极少有人走，对吧？”我问道。

“没几个人走，”他答道，随即无声地暗笑。我不禁浑身发冷。林间漆黑一团，枝头的树叶在风中窸窣交头接耳。

“这片森林里有恶鬼作祟，”我说道。

“乡野村夫皆这般传闻，”他回道，“但我常年在此游荡，却从未目睹过其真容。”

紧接着他开始谈及黑暗中的诸多诡异凶物，皓月悄然升起，阴影在林木间幽幽游弋。他抬头望了一眼冷月。

“快些！”他说，“我们必须在月上中天之前抵达目的地。”

我们在小道上加快了步伐。

“他们传言，”我说道，“这片林区游荡着一头狼人（werewolf）。”

“或许确有其事，”他说，我们便就此话题争辩良久。

===Original===

“The old women say,” said he, “that if a werewolf is slain while a wolf, then he is slain, but if he is slain as a man, then his half-soul will haunt his slayer forever. But haste thee, the moon nears her zenith.”
---
We came into a small moonlit glade and the stranger stopped.
“Let us pause a while,” said he.
“Nay, let us be gone,” I urged; “I like not this place.”
He laughed without sound.
“Why,” said he, “this is a fair glade. As good as a banquet hall it is, and many times have I feasted here. Ha, ha, ha! Look ye, I will show you a dance.” And he began bounding here and there, anon flinging back his head and laughing silently. Thought I, the man is mad.
As he danced his weird dance I looked about me. *The trail went not on but stopped in the glade.*
“Come,” said I, “we must on. Do you not smell the rank, hairy scent that hovers about the glade? Wolves den here. Perhaps they are about us and are gliding upon us even now.”
He dropped upon all fours, bounded higher than my head, and came toward me with a strange slinking motion.
“That dance is called the Dance of the Wolf,” said he, and my hair bristled.
“Keep off!” I stepped back, and with a screech that set the echoes shuddering he leaped for me, and though a sword hung at his belt he did not draw it. My rapier was half out when he grasped my arm and flung me headlong. I dragged him with me and we struck the ground together. Wrenching a hand free I jerked off the mask. A shriek of horror broke from my lips. Beast eyes glittered beneath that mask, white fangs flashed in the moonlight. *The face was that of a wolf.*
In an instant those fangs were at my throat. Taloned hands tore the sword from my grasp. I beat at that horrible face with my clenched fists, but his jaws were fastened on my shoulder, his talons tore at my throat. Then I was on my back. The world was fading. Blindly I struck out. My hand dropped, then closed automatically about the hilt of my dagger, which I had been unable to get at. I drew and stabbed. A terrible, half-bestial bellowing screech. Then I reeled to my feet, free. At my feet lay the werewolf.
I stooped, raised the dagger, then paused, looked up. The moon hovered close to her zenith. *If I slew the thing as a man its frightful spirit would haunt me forever.* I sat down waiting. The *thing* watched me with flaming wolf eyes.

===Chinese===

“老妪们常言，”他说，“倘若一头狼人在化身为狼的原形之时被诛杀，他便算彻底毙命；但倘若他在化作人形之时被杀，他那半人半兽的残魂便将永世缠绕纠祟凶手。不过赶快走吧，月亮快上中天了。”

---

我们步入了一处洒满清冷月华的林间空地，那陌生客驻足停步。

“我们在此歇息片刻吧，”他说。

“不，我们须立刻赶路，”我催促道，“我不喜欢这个鬼地方。”

他又发出一阵无声的冷笑。

“为何，”他说，“此处乃是一座绝佳的空地。比任何宴饮大厅都毫不逊色，我曾无数次在此大快朵颐、痛饮狂欢。哈哈！瞧好了，我给你跳一段舞。”说罢，他开始在空地上四处纵跳翻飞，不时猛地仰起头颅，无声地大笑。我心想，这人定是个疯子。

当他跳起这支怪诞癫狂的诡异舞蹈之际，我机警地环顾四周。小径并未穿过空地向前延伸，而是在这片空地边缘戛然而止！

“快走，”我说道，“我们必须前行。难道你未曾闻见弥漫在空地周遭那股浓烈刺鼻的野兽毛发膻腥气？这里是狼穴。或许狼群已然包围了我们，此刻正悄然向我们潜行逼近！”

他猛地四肢着地仆倒在泥土上，身形一弹纵跳得比我的头顶还要高，随即以一种诡异幽暗的鬼祟姿态朝我蹒跚爬来。

“这支舞蹈名叫‘恶狼之舞’，”他说，听得我头皮发麻、寒毛倒竖。

“退后！”我后退一步拔剑；伴随着一声令山谷回声为之战栗的尖啸，他向我狂扑而来——尽管他腰间挎着利剑，却根本不曾拔出！我的细剑方才拔出一半，他已一把死死攥住我的臂膀，将我狠狠掀翻在地。我顺势将他一同拽倒，二人同时重重砸在地面上。我奋力挣脱出一只手，猛地扯下了他的面具。一声极度惊骇的凄厉惨叫自我的唇间夺眶而出！在那面具之下闪烁着野兽的凶残眼眸，森森白牙在惨白月光下泛着森冷寒芒。那张面孔，竟是不折不扣的狼首！

电光石火间，森白的獠牙已直冲我的咽喉咬来！生着锋利尖爪的兽掌硬生生将长剑从我手中夺走。我用攥紧的拳头拼命狂轰那张狰狞可怖的狼脸，但他的獠牙已狠狠咬合在我的肩头，利爪正疯狂撕扯着我的喉咙。我仰面被压制在地，天地万物在视线中渐渐发黑消逝。我盲目地四下乱抓反抗。右手颓然滑落，随即下意识地死死握住了此前一直无法抽出的腰间匕首剑柄。我闪电般拔出匕首，悍然直刺！一声凄厉刺耳、半人半兽的绝望狂啸破空炸响。我踉跄挣扎着爬起身，重获生天。而在我的脚下，那头狼人正瘫倒在地。

我俯下身，扬起匕首欲补上致命一击，随即猛然一顿，抬头仰望。苍穹上的冷月正堪堪悬挂在中天之畔。倘若我在它化作人形之际将其诛杀，它那可怖的怨魂必将永世纠缠索命！我席地而坐，静静等待。那妖物用一双喷涌着烈焰的凶残狼眼死死盯着我。

===Original===

The long wiry limbs seemed to shrink, to crook; hair seemed to grow upon them. Fearing madness, I snatched up the *thing’s* own sword and hacked it to pieces. Then I flung the sword away and fled.

===Chinese===

那修长精悍的四肢仿佛在收缩、弯折；粗硬的毛发似乎在皮肉上疯狂滋长。因深恐神智发狂陷入癫乱，我一把夺过那妖物遗落的宝剑，将它疯狂乱刃剁成了肉泥碎块。随后我一把将染血的凶剑远远抛开，拔足狂逃。

### I / 一
===Original===

> The Caesar lolled on his ivory throne﻿—

His iron legions came

To break a king in a land unknown,

And a race without a name.

===Chinese===

> 凯撒高卧于象牙王座——
> 铁血军团席卷而来，
> 誓要将无名穷荒之王碾碎，
> 踏灭一个无名无姓的部族。

===Original===

The dagger flashed downward. A sharp cry broke in a gasp. The form on the rough altar twitched convulsively and lay still. The jagged flint edge sawed at the crimsoned breast, and thin bony fingers, ghastly dyed, tore out the still twitching heart. Under matted white brows, sharp eyes gleamed with a ferocious intensity.
Besides the slayer, four men stood about the crude pile of stones that formed the altar of the God of Shadows. One was of medium height, lithely built, scantily clad, whose black hair was confined by a narrow iron band in the center of which gleamed a single red jewel. Of the others, two were dark like the first. But where he was lithe, they were stocky and misshapen, with knotted limbs, and tangled hair falling over sloping brows. His face denoted intelligence and implacable will; theirs merely a beast-like ferocity. The fourth man had little in common with the rest. Nearly a head taller, though his hair was black as theirs, his skin was comparatively light and he was gray-eyed. He eyed the proceedings with little favor.
And, in truth, Cormac of Connacht was little at ease. The Druids of his own isle of Erin had strange dark rites of worship, but nothing like this. Dark trees shut in this grim scene, lit by a single torch. Through the branches moaned an eerie night-wind. Cormac was alone among men of a strange race and he had just seen the heart of a man ripped from his still pulsing body. Now the ancient priest, who looked scarcely human, was glaring at the throbbing thing. Cormac shuddered, glancing at him who wore the jewel. Did Bran Mak Morn, king of the Picts, believe that this white-bearded old butcher could foretell events by scanning a bleeding human heart? The dark eyes of the king were inscrutable. There were strange depths to the man that Cormac could not fathom, nor any other man.
“The portents are good!” exclaimed the priest wildly, speaking more to the two chieftains than to Bran. “Here from the pulsing heart of a captive Roman I read﻿—defeat for the arms of Rome! Triumph for the sons of the heather!”
The two savages murmured beneath their breath, their fierce eyes smoldering.
“Go and prepare your clans for battle,” said the king, and they lumbered away with the apelike gait assumed by such stunted giants. Paying no more heed to the priest who was examining the ghastly ruin on the altar, Bran beckoned to Cormac.

===Chinese===

匕首闪电般向下劈落。一声凄厉的尖叫骤然断绝为急促的哽咽。粗糙祭坛上的躯体剧烈抽搐了几下，便彻底瘫软不动。犬牙交错的燧石边缘锯割着猩红浸透的胸膛，骨瘦如柴的十指染得惨白骇人，硬生生掏出了那颗尚在剧烈搏动的心脏。在乱蓬蓬的白眉之下，一双鹰隼般的厉眸正喷薄出凶残炽烈的精光。

除了刽子手外，粗石垒砌的阴影之神（God of Shadows）祭坛四周尚肃立着四名男子。一人身形中等，体魄精悍柔韧，衣着简陋，乌黑的长发被一根窄细的铁箍束缚在额前，正中镶嵌着一枚熠熠生辉的赤红宝石。另外二人与他一般肤色黧黑；但比起前者的矫健，这两人却矮壮粗苯、体格畸形，四肢虬结如铁，凌乱的毛发低低垂过倾斜前凸的额头。首领的面庞透着聪慧与不可动摇的如铁意志；而这二人的眉宇间，唯有近乎野兽般的野蛮凶残。第四人则与周遭诸人格格不入。他比旁人足足高出近一个头颅，尽管头发同他们一样黑如点漆，肤色却相对白皙，生就一双冷峻的灰眸。他冷眼旁观着眼前的血祭，脸上毫无激赏之色。

实情而言，康诺特的科尔马克（Cormac of Connacht）心中颇为惴惴难安。他故土埃林岛（Erin，爱尔兰）上的德鲁伊（Druids）祭司固然亦有种种阴暗诡谲的秘仪，却绝无眼前这般可怖血腥。阴森漆黑的林木将这处仅由一柄火把照亮的凄惨场景死死圈闭。头顶的枝桠间吹拂过阵阵令人脊背发凉的幽怨夜风。科尔马克孤身一人置身于异族人海之中，方才更是亲眼目睹一颗活生生的人心自犹在搏动的活人体内被硬生生剜出。此刻，那位宛如非人老怪的古老祭司，正贪婪狂暴地审视着那团抽搐的热肉。科尔马克打了个寒战，目光不由投向那位佩戴红宝石的王者。皮克特人之王（King of the Picts）布兰·马克·莫恩（Bran Mak Morn），难道当真相信这个白须屠夫能通过端详一颗血淋淋的人心来预知吉凶成败？年轻国王那双幽邃的黑眸深不可测。在此人身上有着某种科尔马克乃至尘世任何人都无法参透的晦涩渊薮。

“预兆大吉！”祭司发狂般嘶声尖叫，其话语与其说是向布兰禀告，毋宁说是冲着那两名部族酋长呼喊，“自这名受俘罗马战卒尚在搏动的心脏之中，老夫读出了——罗马军团的覆灭！石楠荒原之子的狂胜！”

两名野蛮巨汉在喉底发出阵阵粗浊的低语，凶残的眼眸中翻涌着嗜血的烈焰。

“去集结你们的部族，备战破敌，”国王沉声吩咐，二人随即迈动那矮壮巨人特有的猿猴般步伐，摇摇晃晃地蹒跚离去。布兰不再理睬仍在祭坛上细细端详那堆可怖血肉的祭司，抬手向科尔马克示意。

===Original===

The Gael followed him with alacrity. Once out of that grim grove, under the starlight, he breathed more freely. They stood on an eminence, looking out over long swelling undulations of gently waving heather. Near at hand a few fires twinkled, their fewness giving scant evidence of the hordes of tribesmen who lay close by. Beyond these were more fires and beyond these still more, which last marked the camp of Cormac’s own men, hard-riding, hard-fighting Gaels, who were of that band which was just beginning to get a foothold on the western coast of Caledonia﻿—the nucleus of what was later to become the kingdom of Dalriadia. To the left of these, other fires gleamed.
And far away to the south were more fires﻿—mere pinpoints of light. But even at that distance the Pictish king and his Celtic ally could see that these fires were laid out in regular order.
“The fires of the legions,” muttered Bran. “The fires that have lit a path around the world. The men who light those fires have trampled the races under their iron heels. And now﻿—we of the heather have our backs at the wall. What will fall on the morrow?”
“Victory for us, says the priest,” answered Cormac.
Bran made an impatient gesture. “Moonlight on the ocean. Wind in the fir tops. Do you think that I put faith in such mummery? Or that I enjoyed the butchery of a captive legionary? I must hearten my people; it was for Gron and Bocah that I let old Gonar read the portents. The warriors will fight better.”
“And Gonar?”
Bran laughed. “Gonar is too old to believe﻿—anything. He was high priest of the Shadows a score of years before I was born. He claims direct descent from that Gonar who was a wizard in the days of Brule, the Spear-slayer who was the first of my line. No man knows how old he is﻿—sometimes I think he is the original Gonar himself!”
“At least,” said a mocking voice, and Cormac started as a dim shape appeared at his side, “at least I have learned that in order to keep the faith and trust of the people, a wise man must appear to be a fool. I know secrets that would blast even your brain, Bran, should I speak them. But in order that the people may believe in me, I must descend to such things as they think proper magic﻿—and prance and yell and rattle snakeskins, and dabble about in human blood and chicken livers.”
Cormac looked at the ancient with new interest.

===Chinese===

这位盖尔人（Gael）忙不迭地紧随其后。一旦脱离那座阴森死寂的祭祀密林、重新伫立在浩瀚星空之下，他的呼吸方才稍觉顺畅。他们伫立在高坡之上，放眼远眺，下方是一望无际、在夜风中如波涛般轻柔起伏的辽阔石楠荒原。近处散落着寥寥数点营火，微弱的火光丝毫看不出其间正潜伏着漫山遍野的蛮族战团。越过这些微光，更远处有更多的火光，而在那最远端的一片火海，则是科尔马克麾下健儿的宿营地——一群跃马横刀、骁勇善战的盖尔精骑，正是这支先锋军团刚刚在喀里多尼亚（Caledonia，苏格兰）西海岸站稳脚跟，成为日后达尔里阿达王国（Dalriadia）的雏形骨干。在那营地左侧，另有一片营火在黑夜中闪烁。

而在极目正南方的遥远地平线上，散布着更为浩瀚的营火——宛如针尖般密密麻麻的光点。但即便相隔如此遥远，这位皮克特国王与其凯尔特盟友依然能清晰看出，那些营火排列得井然有序、壁垒森严。

“那是罗马军团的军火，”布兰喃喃低语，“正是那些烈火，照亮了他们踏平尘世四海的征服之路。点燃那些军火的虎狼之师，曾将一个个种族踏灭在铁蹄之下。而如今——我们这群荒原之民已退无可退、背水一战。明日拂晓，等待我们的又将是何等命运？”

“祭司断言大捷必归于我等，”科尔马克答道。

布兰不耐烦地挥了挥手：“不过是海面上的碎月流光，冷杉梢头的过眼阴风。难道你当真以为我深信这等跳梁小丑的巫蛊障眼法？难道你以为我享受将一名被俘军团士兵活生生开膛破肚？我必须振奋我族人的士气；若非为了让格隆（Gron）和博卡赫（Bocah）安心，我何苦让老戈纳尔（Gonar）去占卜吉凶。唯有如此，战士们方能舍生忘死。”

“那老戈纳尔自己呢？”

布兰冷笑一声：“戈纳尔年岁太老，老得早已不再相信世间任何事物。早在我降生二十年前，他便已是阴影祭坛的大祭司。他自称乃是第一代戈纳尔的直系嫡传后裔——那位早在布鲁尔·矛杀者（Brule, the Spear-slayer）、亦即我这一脉始祖的远古蛮荒时代便已名震天下的太古大巫。无人知晓这老怪究竟活了多少寿数——有时我甚至怀疑，他根本就是当年那位戈纳尔本尊！”

“至少，”一个带着戏谑嘲弄的阴冷嗓音骤然响起，引得科尔马克浑身一震，只见一道模糊的虚影已如鬼魅般悄然浮现在他身旁，“至少老朽早已参透，若想维系生民万姓的敬畏与笃信，大智者必先显大愚之相。布兰，老朽所通晓的太古秘辛，若吐露片语，顷刻便能将你的脑髓化作齑粉。然而为了让凡夫愚妇心悦诚服，老朽唯有屈尊降贵去操弄他们心目中认定的正宗巫法——手舞足蹈、凄厉嚎叫、摇晃蛇皮骨殖，将双手浸泡在活人热血与雏鸡肚肠之间。”

科尔马克以一种全新的惊诧目光重新打量着这位行将就木的古怪老者。

===Original===

The semi-madness of his appearance had vanished. He was no longer the charlatan, the spell-mumbling shaman. The starlight lent him a dignity which seemed to increase his very height, so that he stood like a white-bearded patriarch.
“Bran, your doubt lies there.” The lean arm pointed to the fourth ring of fires.
“Aye,” the king nodded gloomily. “Cormac﻿—you know as well as I. Tomorrow’s battle hinges upon that circle of fires. With the chariots of the Britons and your own Western horsemen, our success would be certain, but﻿—surely the devil himself is in the heart of every Northman! You know how I trapped that band﻿—how they swore to fight for me against Rome! And now that their chief, Rognar, is dead, they swear that they will be led only by a king of their own race! Else they will break their vow and go over to the Romans. Without them we are doomed, for we can not change our former plan.”
“Take heart, Bran,” said Gonar. “Touch the jewel in your iron crown. Mayhap it will bring you aid.”
Bran laughed bitterly. “Now you talk as the people think. I am no fool to twist with empty words. What of the gem? It is a strange one, truth, and has brought me luck ere now. But I need now, no jewels, but the allegiance of three hundred fickle Northmen who are the only warriors among us who may stand the charge of the legions on foot.”
“But the jewel, Bran, the jewel!” persisted Gonar.
“Well, the jewel!” cried Bran impatiently. “It is older than this world. It was old when Atlantis and Lemuria sank into the sea. It was given to Brule, the Spear-slayer, first of my line, by the Atlantean Kull, king of Valusia, in the days when the world was young. But shall that profit us now?”
“Who knows?” asked the wizard obliquely. “Time and space exist not. There was no past, and there shall be no future. **Now** is all. All things that ever were, are, or ever will be, transpire *now*. Man is forever at the center of what we call time and space. I have gone into yesterday and tomorrow and both were as real as today﻿—which is like the dreams of ghosts! But let me sleep and talk with Gonar. Mayhap he shall aid us.”
“What means he?” asked Cormac, with a slight twitching of his shoulders, as the priest strode away in the shadows.
“He has ever said that the first Gonar comes to him in his dreams and talks with him,” answered Bran.

===Chinese===

他身上先前显露出的半疯疯癫之态早已荡然无存。他不再是那个装神弄鬼、口吐含混咒语的江湖萨满。漫天星辉洒在他周身，平添了一股不怒自威的庄严，仿佛连他的身量都在瞬间暴涨，巍然如一位白髯飘拂的远古宗族元老。

“布兰，你心头的疑虑悬于彼处。”那干枯如柴的手臂径直指向第四圈营火。

“不错，”国王阴沉地颔首，“科尔马克——你我心知肚明。明日之战的胜负天平，全系于那处营火圈。若有不列颠人的双轮战车，再加上你麾下西方健儿的纵横铁骑，我军胜算操之在我；然而——那群北方蛮子（Northman）每一个人的心窍里，皆盘踞着恶魔！你深知我当初是如何将那帮贼寇围困在绝地——他们又是如何起誓愿为我死战抗击罗马！如今他们的首领罗格纳（Rognar）阵亡，这群恶狼竟叫嚣唯有本族的王者方配统领他们！否则他们宁可撕毁誓言，投奔罗马阵营。若失了他们，我等必败无疑，因先前定下的破阵军略已无从更改。”

“振作起来，布兰，”戈纳尔说道，“抚摸你铁冠上的那枚红宝石吧。或许它能为你引来神助。”

布兰苦涩地冷笑：“如今你说话倒又像那些愚夫愚妇一般了。我绝非能被三两句虚妄言辞所糊弄的蠢汉。宝石又当如何？诚然，那是一枚通灵异宝，昔日亦曾为我带来气运。但眼下我需要的绝非奇石珍宝，而是那三百名反复无常的北方狂战士的生死效忠——在这漫山遍野的联军之中，唯有他们能在步战中硬抗罗马军团的钢铁重步兵方阵冲锋！”

“但正是那枚宝石，布兰，那枚宝石！”戈纳尔执拗地坚持道。

“够了，那宝石又如何！”布兰不耐烦地低吼，“它比这方尘世还要古老苍凉。早在亚特兰蒂斯（Atlantis）与利莫里亚（Lemuria）沉入万丈狂澜之前，它便已然亘古永存。那是在天地玄黄的洪荒时代，瓦卢西亚之王（King of Valusia）、亚特兰蒂斯人库尔（Kull），亲手赐予我这一脉始祖布鲁尔·矛杀者的圣物。但那古物在今朝又能救得了谁？”

“谁又能料定乾坤？”大巫意味深长地反问道，“时间与空间本属虚妄幻灭。世间本无过去，亦无未来。唯有‘当下’乃是万物之总纲。天地间曾有、现有、将有之一切造化，皆在此刻‘当下’同时生灭交织。人，永远伫立在所谓的时空正枢纽之中。我曾漫游至昔日昨天，亦曾涉足至明日来者，二者皆如今日一般真实分明——而今日亦不过如幽灵之幻梦！且容老朽阖眼入梦，去与戈纳尔一叙。或许他能助我等一臂之力。”

“他究竟是何意？”随着祭司隐入幽暗阴影，科尔马克双肩不由自主地抽搐了一下，低声问道。

“他向来坚称，太古第一代戈纳尔时常在梦境中降临与他促膝长谈，”布兰答道。

===Original===

“I have seen him perform deeds that seemed beyond human ken. I know not. I am but an unknown king with an iron crown, trying to lift a race of savages out of the slime into which they have sunk. Let us look to the camps.”
---
As they walked Cormac wondered. By what strange freak of fate had such a man risen among this race of savages, survivors of a darker, grimmer age? Surely he was an atavism, an original type of the days when the Picts ruled all Europe, before their primitive empire fell before the bronze swords of the Gauls. Cormac knew how Bran, rising by his own efforts from the negligent position of the son of a Wolf clan chief, had to an extent united the tribes of the heather and now claimed kingship over all Caledon. But his rule was loose and much remained before the Pictish clans would forget their feuds and present a solid front to foreign foes. On the battle of the morrow, the first pitched battle between the Picts under their king and the Romans, hinged the future of the rising Pictish kingdom.
Bran and his ally walked through the Pictish camp where the swart warriors lay sprawled about their small fires, sleeping or gnawing half-cooked food. Cormac was impressed by their silence. A thousand men camped here, yet the only sounds were occasional low guttural intonations. The silence of the Stone Age rested in the souls of these men.
They were all short﻿—most of them crooked of limb. Giant dwarfs; Bran Mak Morn was a tall man among them. Only the older men were bearded and they scantily, but their black hair fell about their eyes so that they peered fiercely from under the tangle. They were barefoot and clad scantily in wolf-skins. Their arms consisted in short barbed swords of iron, heavy black bows, arrows tipped with flint, iron and copper, and stone-headed mallets. Defensive armor they had none, save for a crude shield of hide-covered wood; many had worked bits of metal into their tangled manes as a slight protection against sword-cuts. Some few, sons of long lines of chiefs, were smooth-limbed and lithe like Bran, but in the eyes of all gleamed the unquenchable savagery of the primeval.
These men are fully savages, thought Cormac, worse than the Gauls, Britons and Germans. Can the old legends be true﻿—that they reigned in a day when strange cities rose where now the sea rolls?

===Chinese===

“我曾亲眼目睹他施展过诸多超越凡人智慧极限的通神异术。我参不透。我不过是一个头戴铁冠的卑微王者，正竭尽全力试图将一个野蛮部族从他们深陷其中的蒙昧泥淖中拉拔出来。且随我巡视各处军营吧。”

---

并肩漫步之际，科尔马克心潮起伏。究竟是何等诡谲莫测的宿命机缘，竟让这群历经幽暗冷酷纪元残存至今的蛮荒遗民之中，诞生了这般雄才大略的绝世霸主？他分明是一种奇迹般的返祖之象，生就当年皮克特人横扫整个欧罗巴大陆、其原始帝国尚未在被高卢人的青铜利剑踏碎之前的上古王者气度。科尔马克深知，布兰仅凭自身过人的铁血手腕，从狼氏族（Wolf clan）酋长之子的卑微地位崛起，在极大程度上整合了石楠荒原上的散乱部族，如今更是自封为全喀里多尼亚之王。然而他的王权依然松散羸弱，若想让各皮克特氏族彻底摒弃世代仇杀、筑起对抗外侮的铁壁铜墙，前路尚有千难万险。明日之战，作为皮克特人在统一王者统帅下与罗马帝国展开的首次正面对决，将彻底决定这个新兴蛮族王国的存亡命运。

布兰与其盟友穿行于皮克特人的营盘之间，黧黑精悍的战士们三五成群地散卧在微弱的营火周遭，或沉沉酣睡，或撕咬着半生不熟的肉块。令科尔马克大为震撼的，是他们死一般的沉默。千名狂徒扎营于此，周遭唯有偶尔断续的低沉喉音在黑夜中回荡。石器时代的无边死寂，已然深深刻入这群蛮荒狂夫的骨髓之中。

他们身材矮小——多数四肢粗短畸形。俨然是一群孔武有力的侏儒巨汉；在他们之中，布兰·马克·莫恩已算得上身材修长挺拔的高个子。唯有年迈的长者方蓄有稀疏的短须，但漆黑浓密的乱发低低遮掩着双目，令他们在蓬乱的发丛下投射出如野兽般凶残警觉的光芒。他们赤足坦胸，仅用狼皮简陋裹体。他们的兵刃由粗糙短小的带钩铁剑、沉重黝黑的角弓、以燧石、精铁或熟铜磨制的羽箭，以及硕大的石槌组成。防身甲胄他们全无半件，唯有一面生皮包裹木板的简陋圆盾；许多人将零星的碎铜烂铁编入蓬乱的乱发之中，以此作为抵御刀劈剑砍的微薄防护。唯有少数历代大酋长骨血相传的嫡系后裔，方如布兰一般四肢修长匀称，然而在每一个人的眼眸深处，皆燃烧着史前蛮荒永不熄灭的凶残烈焰。

这群人乃是不折不扣的原始蛮夷，科尔马克暗自思忖，比高卢人、不列颠人乃至日耳曼人还要野蛮粗暴。难道古老的先人传说当真不虚——在今日狂澜滔天的怒海之下，当年曾矗立着他们亲手建立的奇迹城垣？

===Original===

And that they survived the flood that washed those gleaming empires under, sinking again into that savagery from which they once had risen?
Close to the encampment of the tribesmen were the fires of a group of Britons﻿—members of fierce tribes who lived south of the Roman Wall but who dwelt in the hills and forests to the west and defied the power of Rome. Powerfully built men they were, with blazing blue eyes and shocks of tousled yellow hair, such men as had thronged the Ceanntish beaches when Caesar brought the Eagles into the Isles. These men, like the Picts, wore no armor, and were clad scantily in coarse-worked cloth and deerskin sandals. They bore small round bucklers of hard wood, braced with bronze, to be worn on the left arm, and long heavy bronze swords with blunt points. Some had bows, though the Britons were indifferent archers. Their bows were shorter than the Picts’ and effective only at close range. But ranged close by their fires were the weapons that had made the name Briton a word of terror to Pict, Roman and Norse raider alike. Within the circle of firelight stood fifty bronze chariots with long cruel blades curving out from the sides. One of these blades could dismember half a dozen men at once. Tethered close by under the vigilant eyes of their guards grazed the chariot horses﻿—big, rangy steeds, swift and powerful.
“Would that we had more of them!” mused Bran. “With a thousand chariots and my bowmen I could drive the legions into the sea.”
“The free British tribes must eventually fall before Rome,” said Cormac. “It would seem they would rush to join you in your war.”
Bran made a helpless gesture. “The fickleness of the Celt. They can not forget old feuds. Our ancient men have told us how they would not even unite against Caesar when the Romans first came. They will not make head against a common foe together. These men came to me because of some dispute with their chief, but I can not depend on them when they are not actually fighting.”
Cormac nodded. “I know; Caesar conquered Gaul by playing one tribe against another. My own people shift and change with the waxing and waning of the tides. But of all Celts, the Cymry are the most changeable, the least stable.

===Chinese===

难道他们当真是在那场将辉煌帝国尽数吞没灭顶的滔天大洪水中侥幸生还，随后重新堕入昔日好不容易挣脱而出的蛮荒泥沼之中？

紧邻皮克特人营寨之旁，燃烧着一群不列颠人的营火——这群桀骜不驯的凶悍蛮族居住在罗马长城（Roman Wall）以南，却隐匿在西部的崇山峻岭与幽林深处，公然抗拒罗马的铁血霸权。这群汉子生得魁梧雄壮，生就一双喷涌烈焰的碧蓝眼眸，顶着一头蓬乱的金黄长发，恰如当年凯撒将鹰旗插上不列颠群岛之际、蜂拥阻击在肯特海岸边的怒海狂涛。这群人同皮克特人一样不着甲胄，仅着粗劣麻布短袍与鹿皮草履。他们左臂挎着以坚木制成、镶嵌熟铜的轻巧圆盾，手中握着长重而剑尖钝圆的青铜巨剑。少数人携有短弓，但不列颠人向来拙于射术。他们的木弓比皮克特人的长弓更为短小，唯在近距离搏杀中方能奏效。然而紧挨着他们营火肃立的杀阵利器，却曾令皮克特人、罗马军团乃至北欧海盗闻风丧胆。在营火光影的环抱中，肃然陈列着五十辆青铜战车，两侧探出修长冰冷、弧度残忍的锋利月牙弯刀。单单一柄飞旋的利刃，便能在疾驰中瞬间将半打血肉之躯拦腰斩断。在守卫警惕的注视下，挽马正系在近旁安然嚼草——一匹匹骨骼粗壮、体格高大的骏马，奔腾迅疾而神力惊人。

“但愿我们能拥有更多这般战车！”布兰若有所思地叹息道，“若有一千辆飞刀战车配合我的石楠弓手，我顷刻便能将罗马军团赶下怒海。”

“不列颠的自由氏族终究难逃覆亡于罗马的宿命，”科尔马克说道，“照理他们本该争先恐后奔赴你的麾下共举义旗。”

布兰无可奈何地摊开双手：“此乃凯尔特人的天性寡信与轻浮。他们永远忘不掉往昔的族内私仇。我族的先贤曾告诫我们，当年罗马人初叩门户之际，他们甚至不肯联合御敌共抗凯撒。面对大军压境的强敌，他们从不肯携手并肩。眼前这批健儿之所以投奔于我，不过是因与自家酋长生了嫌隙；若非身处真刀真枪的修罗战场，我根本无法对他们寄予厚望。”

科尔马克微微颔首：“我何尝不知；当年凯撒征服高卢，正是借由挑拨离间使各部互相倾轧之计。我自己的族人亦如潮汐盈亏般朝秦暮楚。然而在所有凯尔特人之中，库姆里人（Cymry）最为反复无常、心性最为浮躁难定。

===Original===

Not many centuries ago my own Gaelic ancestors wrested Erin from the Cymric Danaans, because though they outnumbered us, they opposed us as separate tribes, rather than as a nation.”
“And so these Cymric Britons face Rome,” said Bran. “These will aid us on the morrow. Further I can not say. But how shall I expect loyalty from alien tribes, who am not sure of my own people? Thousands lurk in the hills, holding aloof. I am king in name only. Let me win tomorrow and they will flock to my standard; if I lose, they will scatter like birds before a cold wind.”
---
A chorus of rough welcome greeted the two leaders as they entered the camp of Cormac’s Gaels. Five hundred in number they were, tall rangy men, black-haired and gray-eyed mainly, with the bearing of men who lived by war alone. While there was nothing like close discipline among them, there was an air of more system and practical order than existed in the lines of the Picts and Britons. These men were of the last Celtic race to invade the Isles and their barbaric civilization was of much higher order than that of their Cymric kin. The ancestors of the Gaels had learned the arts of war on the vast plains of Scythia and at the courts of the Pharaohs where they had fought as mercenaries of Egypt, and much of what they learned they brought into Ireland with them. Excelling in metal work, they were armed, not with clumsy bronze swords, but with high-grade weapons of iron.
They were clad in well-woven kilts and leathern sandals. Each wore a light shirt of chain mail and a visorless helmet, but this was all of their defensive armor. Celts, Gaelic or Brythonic, were prone to judge a man’s valor by the amount of armor he wore. The Britons who faced Caesar deemed the Romans cowards because they cased themselves in metal, and many centuries later the Irish clans thought the same of the mail-clad Norman knights of Strongbow.
Cormac’s warriors were horsemen. They neither knew nor esteemed the use of the bow. They bore the inevitable round, metal-braced buckler, dirks, long straight swords and light single-handed axes. Their tethered horses grazed not far away﻿—big-boned animals, not so ponderous as those raised by the Britons, but swifter.
Bran’s eyes lighted as the two strode through the camp. “These men are keen-beaked birds of war! See how they whet their axes and jest of the morrow!

===Chinese===

“就在数个世纪之前，我麾下盖尔先祖硬生生从库姆里达南族（Cymric Danaans）手中夺下了爱尔兰，正是因为他们兵力虽众，却各自为战互不相救，而非拧成一股绳的统一王国。”

“而如今这群库姆里不列颠人正面临罗马铁蹄，”布兰说道，“明日之战他们自会竭力死战。至于往后，我不敢妄断。然而我连自家骨肉同胞的忠诚尚且难保，又怎敢奢求异族部落矢志不渝？数以千计的皮克特勇士至今仍躲藏在群山之间作壁上观。我眼下不过是个挂名的虚妄君王。明日倘若功成大捷，他们自会蜂拥奔赴王旗之下；明日若有一朝败绩，他们便会如寒风惊鸟般四散飞逃。”

---

当两位领袖迈入科尔马克麾下盖尔骑兵的营地时，一阵粗豪热烈的欢呼声扑面而来。这支大军足有五百精锐，个个身材修长精悍，多数黑发灰眸，举手投足间皆透着纯粹刀口舔血者的铁血煞气。尽管他们队列之间全无森严的条理纪律，但比起皮克特人与不列颠人的混乱营盘，此处分明流淌着一股更具章法与实战秩序的行伍风范。这群健儿属于最后一批踏平不列颠群岛的凯尔特征服者，其蛮荒文明的高度远胜过其库姆里远亲。盖尔先祖曾驰骋于斯基泰（Scythia）的浩瀚草原，曾充当法老王宫廷之中的埃及雇佣军，在刀山火海中习得了战争的高深机略，并将其精髓全盘带入了爱尔兰。他们精于冶金锻造，手中挥舞的绝非笨重的青铜短兵，而是千锤百炼的高纯精铁锋刃。

他们身着裁剪利落的苏格兰褶裙（kilts）与生皮草履。每人皆披一件轻便锁子甲短衫，头戴无面罩精铁胄，这便是他们全部的防御重铠。不论是盖尔还是布立吞凯尔特人，皆倾向于以披挂甲胄的厚薄来衡量一个汉子的血性胆识。当年阻击凯撒的不列颠先民将身披重甲的罗马军团斥为懦夫，而数个世纪之后爱尔兰氏族面对斯特朗鲍（Strongbow）麾下披坚执锐的诺曼骑士时，亦怀抱着完全如出一辙的傲慢轻蔑。

科尔马克麾下的武士皆是纵横驰骋的骁勇骑兵。他们既不谙射术，亦向来鄙夷弓矢。他们左手挽着必不可少的铜边轻巧圆盾，腰跨匕首、笔直修长的阔刃铁剑，以及轻巧单手战斧。战马系在不远处的草滩上悠然嚼草——骨架庞大，虽不如不列颠战马那般沉重如牛，却迅疾如电、野性难驯。

穿行于营地之中，布兰的双眸不由自主地熠熠生辉：“这群汉子真乃翱翔战阵之上的凶禽铁鹰！且看他们是如何磨砺战斧、谈笑明日血战的！”

===Original===

Would that the raiders in yon camp were as staunch as your men, Cormac! Then would I greet the legions with a laugh when they come up from the south tomorrow.”
They were entering the circle of the Northmen fires. Three hundred men sat about gambling, whetting their weapons and drinking deep of the heather ale furnished them by their Pictish allies. These gazed upon Bran and Cormac with no great friendliness. It was striking to note the difference between them and the Picts and Celts﻿—the difference in their cold eyes, their strong moody faces, their very bearing. Here was ferocity, and savagery, but not of the wild, upbursting fury of the Celt. Here was fierceness backed by grim determination and stolid stubbornness. The charge of the British clans was terrible, overwhelming. But they had no patience; let them be balked of immediate victory and they were likely to lose heart and scatter or fall to bickering among themselves. There was the patience of the cold blue North in these seafarers﻿—a lasting determination that would keep them steadfast to the bitter end, once their face was set toward a definite goal.
As to personal stature, they were giants; massive yet rangy. That they did not share the ideas of the Celts regarding armor was shown by the fact that they were clad in heavy scale-mail shirts that reached below mid-thigh, heavy horned helmets and hardened hide leggings, reinforced, as were their shoes, with plates of iron. Their shields were huge oval affairs of hard wood, hide and brass. As to weapons, they had long iron-headed spears, heavy iron axes, and daggers. Some had long wide-bladed swords.
Cormac scarcely felt at ease with the cold magnetic eyes of these flaxen-haired men fixed upon him. He and they were hereditary foes, even though they did chance to be fighting on the same side at present﻿—but were they?
A man came forward, a tall gaunt warrior on whose scarred, wolfish face the flickering firelight reflected deep shadows. With his wolfskin mantle flung carelessly about his wide shoulders, and the great horns on his helmet adding to his height, he stood there in the swaying shadows, like some half-human thing, a brooding shape of the dark barbarism that was soon to engulf the world.
“Well, Wulfhere,” said the Pictish king, “you have drunk the mead of council and have spoken about the fires﻿—what is your decision?”

===Chinese===

“但愿那座营盘里的掠夺者能如你的健儿一般刚毅如铁，科尔马克！若真如此，明日当军团自南方大举压境之时，我自当纵声狂笑以迎强敌。”

他们举步踏入了北方人的营火圈。三百名狂徒正围坐在火堆周遭聚赌搏戏、磨砺锋刃，豪饮着皮克特盟友奉上的烈性石楠麦酒。这群凶徒投向布兰与科尔马克的目光全无友善可言。他们与皮克特人及凯尔特人之间的天壤之别令人心惊——在那双冷酷的眸子里，在那张刚毅沉郁的面孔上，在那挺拔傲立的体态间，无不彰显着截然不同的本色。此处同样弥漫着凶残与野蛮，却绝非凯尔特人那种狂乱暴烈、骤起骤落的血性狂怒。这是一种以冷酷坚毅与磐石般顽固为底色的彻骨凶悍。不列颠氏族的冲锋狂暴凶残、摧枯拉朽；但他们毫无耐心可言；一旦未能顷刻大捷，他们往往斗志瓦解、四散崩溃，抑或转为族内自相残杀的无休纷争。而在这群踏浪而来的北欧狂客身上，却浸透着极北幽蓝冻土的亘古沉静——一种一旦认准既定目标便誓死血战至最后一息的百折不挠。

论及身量，他们尽皆是巨人；魁伟雄壮而骨骼宽大。他们绝不认同凯尔特人轻视铠甲的迂腐观念，身上整齐披挂着下垂过大腿中段的厚重鳞甲重衫，头戴插角重盔，双腿缚着坚韧硬皮护胫，鞋靴与绑腿上皆加固着沉重铁板。他们的巨盾乃是坚木、生皮与黄铜铸就的硕大椭圆重盾。至于兵刃，则配有长柄铁矛、沉重阔刃战斧与锋利匕首；更有许多人挎着宽刃双手大剑。

被这群金发巨汉冰冷而充满压迫感的眼眸死死锁定，科尔马克心头颇感压抑。尽管眼下机缘巧合结为同盟，他们彼此却是不折不扣的宿世死敌——然而，他们当真算是同盟吗？

一名汉子自人群中排众而出，那是一位身材瘦削高大的枯槁战士，跳跃的营火在他那刀疤纵横、宛如饿狼般的面庞上投下深邃阴森的浓重阴影。一领狼皮大氅漫不经心地披散在他宽阔如门板的双肩上，战盔上耸立的巨大牛角更平添了几分凶悍煞气；他伫立在晃动的火影之中，宛如某种半人半兽的太古凶物，俨然是一具即将把整座凡俗世界拖入灭顶泥淖之漆黑蛮荒的化身。

“如何，伍尔夫赫尔（Wulfhere），”皮克特国王开口道，“议事的蜜酒既已入腹，火堆旁的谋算亦已落定——你们的决断究竟为何？”

===Original===

The Northman’s eyes flashed in the gloom. “Give us a king of our own race to follow if you wish us to fight for you.”
Bran flung out his hands. “Ask me to drag down the stars to gem your helmets! Will not your comrades follow you?”
“Not against the legions,” answered Wulfhere sullenly. “A king led us on the viking path﻿—a king must lead us against the Romans. And Rognar is dead.”
“I am a king,” said Bran. “Will you fight for me if I stand at the tip of your fight wedge?”
“A king of our own race,” said Wulfhere doggedly. “We are all picked men of the North. We fight for none but a king, and a king must lead us﻿—against the legions.”
Cormac sensed a subtle threat in this repeated phrase.
“Here is a prince of Erin,” said Bran. “Will you fight for the Westerner?”
“We fight under no Celt, West or East,” growled the viking, and a low rumble of approval rose from the onlookers. “It is enough to fight by their side.”
The hot Gaelic blood rose in Cormac’s brain and he pushed past Bran, his hand on his sword. “How mean you that, pirate?”
Before Wulfhere could reply Bran interposed: “Have done! Will you fools throw away the battle before it is fought, by your madness? What of your oath, Wulfhere?”
“We swore it under Rognar; when he died from a Roman arrow we were absolved of it. We will follow only a king﻿—against the legions.”
“But your comrades will follow you﻿—against the heather people!” snapped Bran.
“Aye,” the Northman’s eyes met his brazenly. “Send us a king or we join the Romans tomorrow.”
Bran snarled. In his rage he dominated the scene, dwarfing the huge men who towered over him.
“Traitors! Liars! I hold your lives in my hand! Aye, draw your swords if you will﻿—Cormac, keep your blade in its sheath. These wolves will not bite a king! Wulfhere﻿—I spared your lives when I could have taken them.
“You came to raid the countries of the South, sweeping down from the northern sea in your galleys. You ravaged the coasts and the smoke of burning villages hung like a cloud over the shores of Caledon. I trapped you all when you were pillaging and burning﻿—with the blood of my people on your hands. I burned your long ships and ambushed you when you followed. With thrice your number of bowmen who burned for your lives hidden in the heathered hills about you, I spared you when we could have shot you down like trapped wolves.

布兰猛地摊开双手：“你干脆让我去把漫天星斗摘下来镶在你们的头盔上算了！难道你的弟兄们连你也不肯听从？”

科尔马克在这反复强调的话音中嗅到了一股赤裸裸的威胁。

狂暴滚烫的盖尔热血瞬间冲上了科尔马克的头顶，他一把推开布兰，右手按住剑柄欺身而上：“海盗贼子，你这话是何意？”

尚未等伍尔夫赫尔还嘴，布兰已横步切入二人之间：“住手！大敌当前，你们两个蠢货难道要用这等疯狂将胜机付诸东流？伍尔夫赫尔，你们当初立下的誓言又当何论？”

“当初我们是对着罗格纳立誓；如今他中了一记罗马暗箭暴毙，我们的誓言自然荡然无存。迎击罗马军团，我们唯随王者冲锋！”

“可若要你们调转刀头对抗石楠荒原的子民，你的弟兄们倒乐意唯你马首是瞻了！”布兰厉声反唇相讥。

“正是，”北方汉子厚颜无耻地直视着国王的双目，“给我们一位同族王者，否则明日拂晓我们便倒戈投奔罗马！”

布兰喉底发出一声暴怒的低吼。在他滔天杀意的震慑之下，整座场面瞬间被他一人死死压制，反倒衬托得周遭高大魁梧的巨汉们相形见绌。

“背信弃义的叛徒！满嘴谎言的懦夫！你们所有人的狗命全捏在我的手心！拔剑啊，有种你们尽管拔剑——科尔马克，把你的利剑留在鞘中！这群野狗绝不敢噬咬一位真正的王者！伍尔夫赫尔——当初我本可将你们赶尽杀绝，是我饶了你们一条狗命！

“你们当年自北方怒海驾着长船破浪南下，洗劫南方各邦。你们屠戮沿海村落，冲天焦黑的狼烟如重云般笼罩在喀里多尼亚海岸线上！当你们满手沾满我族民的热血、大肆劫掠烧杀之际，是我亲手将你们困入绝境！是我一把火烧光了你们的维京长船，在你们疯狂追击之际设伏截杀！方圆石楠丘陵上埋伏着三倍于你们、恨不能饮你们血啖你们肉的石楠神箭手，彼时我们本可将你们像瓮中之鳖般射成刺猬，是我大发慈悲饶了你们一命！”

Because I spared you, you swore to come and fight for me.”
“And shall we die because the Picts fight Rome?” rumbled a bearded raider.
“Your lives are forfeit to me; you came to ravage the South. I did not promise to send you all back to your homes in the North unharmed and loaded with loot. Your vow was to fight one battle against Rome under my standard. Then I will aid your survivors to build ships and you may go where you will, with a goodly share of the plunder we take from the legions. Rognar had kept his oath. But Rognar died in a skirmish with Roman scouts and now you, Wulfhere the Dissension-breeder, you stir up your comrades to dishonor themselves by that which a Northman hates﻿—the breaking of the sworn word.”
“We break no oath,” snarled the viking, and the king sensed the basic Germanic stubbornness, far harder to combat than the fickleness of the fiery Celts. “Give us a king, neither Pict, Gael nor Briton, and we will die for you. If not﻿—then we will fight tomorrow for the greatest of all kings﻿—the emperor of Rome!”

For a moment Cormac thought that the Pictish king, in his black rage, would draw and strike the Northman dead. The concentrated fury that blazed in Bran’s dark eyes caused Wulfhere to recoil and drop a hand to his belt.
“Fool!” said Mak Morn in a low voice that vibrated with passion. “I could sweep you from the earth before the Romans are near enough to hear your death howls. Choose﻿—fight for me on the morrow﻿—or die tonight under a black cloud of arrows, a red storm of swords, a dark wave of chariots!”
At the mention of the chariots, the only arm of war that had ever broken the Norse shield-wall, Wulfhere changed expression, but he held his ground.
“War be it,” he said doggedly. “Or a king to lead us!”
The Northmen responded with a short deep roar and a clash of swords on shields. Bran, eyes blazing, was about to speak again when a white shape glided silently into the ring of firelight.
“Soft words, soft words,” said old Gonar tranquilly. “King, say no more. Wulfhere, you and your fellows will fight for us if you have a king to lead you?”
“We have sworn.”
“Then be at ease,” quoth the wizard; “for ere battle joins on the morrow I will send you such a king as no man on earth has followed for a hundred thousand years! A king neither Pict, Gael nor Briton, but one to whom the emperor of Rome is as but a village headman!”

某一刹那，科尔马克甚至料定皮克特国王会在狂暴的血怒之下霍然拔剑将这贼首当场斩杀。布兰黑眸中喷薄而出的凝练杀机，逼得伍尔夫赫尔不由自主地倒退半步，手掌下意识按向腰带。

“蠢材！”布兰压低嗓音说道，那话音中震颤着足以冻结骨血的杀意，“早在罗马人能听到你们临死惨嚎之前，我便能在瞬息之间将你们全族从大地上彻底抹去！任凭你们挑选——要么明日为我死战——要么今夜便在这漫天蔽日的黑羽箭雨、这猩红暴烈的利刃风暴、这黑压压呼啸碾压的战车狂潮之下死无葬身之地！”

一听提及战车之名——那是世间唯一曾正面撕裂北欧盾墙阵（shield-wall）的屠戮凶器，伍尔夫赫尔的脸色微变，但他依旧咬牙死扛。

“那便血战到底，”他顽固地叫嚣道，“除非给我们一位王者领军！”

北方狂战士们发出一阵低沉有力的咆哮，利剑出鞘狠狠撞击在圆盾上，激起震耳欲聋的金铁交鸣。布兰眼中怒火中烧，正欲拔剑暴喝，一道白色的虚影已悄无声息地滑入了营火圈中央。

“莫动肝火，心平气和，”老戈纳尔神色淡然地开口道，“国王，休要多言。伍尔夫赫尔，只要有一位王者为你们领军，你与你的弟兄便愿为我们赴汤蹈火？”

“我们已然立誓。”

“那便安心歇息去吧，”大巫慢条斯理地说道，“因在明日两军对垒交锋之前，老夫定当为你们引来一位尘世凡人十万年来未尝追随过的旷世霸主！一位既非皮克特、亦非盖尔与不列颠的至尊王者，在他面前，罗马皇帝不过是个微不足道的村野鄙夫罢了！”

While they stood undecided, Gonar took the arms of Cormac and Bran. “Come. And you, Northmen, remember your vow, and my promise which I have never broken. Sleep now, nor think to steal away in the darkness to the Roman camp, for if you escaped our shafts you would not escape either my curse or the suspicions of the legionaries.”
So the three walked away and Cormac, looking back, saw Wulfhere standing by the fire, fingering his golden beard, with a look of puzzled anger on his lean evil face.
---
The three walked silently through the waving heather under the faraway stars while the weird night-wind whispered ghostly secrets about them.
“Ages ago,” said the wizard suddenly, “in the days when the world was young, great lands rose where now the ocean roars. On these lands thronged mighty nations and kingdoms. Greatest of all these was Valusia﻿—Land of Enchantment. Rome is as a village compared to the splendor of the cities of Valusia. And the greatest king was Kull, who came from the land of Atlantis to wrest the crown of Valusia from a degenerate dynasty. The Picts who dwelt in the isles which now form the mountain peaks of a strange land upon the Western Ocean, were allies of Valusia, and the greatest of all the Pictish war-chiefs was Brule the Spear-slayer, first of the line men call Mak Morn.
“Kull gave to Brule the jewel which you now wear in your iron crown, oh king, after a strange battle in a dim land, and down the long ages it has come to us, ever a sign of the Mak Morn, a symbol of former greatness. When at last the sea rose and swallowed Valusia, Atlantis and Lemuria, only the Picts survived and they were scattered and few. Yet they began again the slow climb upward, and though many of the arts of civilization were lost in the great flood, yet they progressed. The art of metalworking was lost, so they excelled in the working of flint. And they ruled all the new lands flung up by the sea and now called Europe, until down from the north came younger tribes who had scarce risen from the ape when Valusia reigned in her glory, and who, dwelling in the icy lands about the Pole, knew naught of the lost splendor of the Seven Empires and little of the flood that had swept away half a world.
“And still they have come﻿—Aryans, Celts, Germans, swarming down from the great cradle of their race which lies near the Pole.

===Chinese===

正当众人惊疑不定、进退维谷之际，戈纳尔已顺势挽住了科尔马克与布兰的手臂：“走吧。至于你们，北方人，切莫忘记你们的血誓，以及老夫平生从未食言过的承诺。安心睡去吧，莫要盘算着借着夜色偷偷溜向罗马大营，因倘若你们侥幸避得开漫天箭矢，亦绝难逃脱老夫的灭顶诅咒，更避不开罗马军团的猜忌屠戮。”

三人随即拂袖而去；科尔马克回首望去，只见伍尔夫赫尔正呆立在营火之旁，骨瘦如柴的阴鸷面庞上交织着困惑与狂怒，手指无意识地摩挲着金黄的长髯。

---

三人默默穿行在波涛起伏的石楠丛中，头顶是浩瀚孤绝的万里繁星，幽暗的夜风在周遭如游魂般窃窃私语，倾诉着太古不可言说的幽暗秘辛。

“在亿万年之前的远古洪荒，”大巫突然幽幽开口，“在天地玄黄的初生纪元，如今狂涛怒吼的汪洋大壑，昔日曾是一片片辽阔无垠的繁盛大陆。在那些大地上，曾繁衍着无数雄霸天下的强盛国度。而在所有帝国之中最为显赫绝伦者，乃是瓦卢西亚（Valusia）——幻梦之国。与瓦卢西亚万千城垣的锦绣繁华相比，今日之罗马不过是座寒酸鄙陋的村落。而在那万国之上最伟大的霸主，乃是自亚特兰蒂斯（Atlantis）破浪而来、自腐朽王朝手中生生夺下瓦卢西亚王冠的库尔（Kull）。昔日栖居于群岛之上、其列岛如今已化作西方大洋上异域崇山峻岭之峰巅的皮克特部族，乃是瓦卢西亚的永恒盟友；而在所有皮克特战阵大酋长之中，最为盖世无双者，当属布鲁尔·矛杀者（Brule the Spear-slayer），亦即世人所称马克·莫恩王脉的开山始祖！

“国王啊，在幽暗异域经历了一场惊天动地的诡异血战之后，库尔亲手将你如今镶嵌在铁冠上的那枚血色宝石赐予了布鲁尔；历经漫长浩瀚的亘古岁月，它代代相传流转至今，永远作为马克·莫恩之尊荣象征，见证着昔日的无上荣耀。及至最终汪洋暴涨、怒海滔天，将瓦卢西亚、亚特兰蒂斯与利莫里亚（Lemuria）尽数沉没吞噬，唯有皮克特一族历经灭顶狂澜侥幸残存，却已凋零流散。然而他们重新开始了步步登高的艰难跋涉；尽管无数灿烂文明的无上技艺皆在那场大洪水中荡然无存，他们依然负重前行。冶金之术已然失传，他们便将燧石雕琢之术演化至极境。他们曾君临统御整座由怒海喷薄而出、如今被称作欧罗巴的大陆，直至北方极寒之地涌下了年轻的部族——当瓦卢西亚在绝世荣耀中傲视寰宇之际，那些部落尚且不过是刚从猿猴蜕变而出的蛮荒生灵，他们幽居在极地冰原之间，对七大帝国湮灭的荣光一无所知，对曾将半个尘世彻底抹去的大洪水亦全无耳闻。

“自此他们便如狂潮般接踵而至——雅利安人、凯尔特人、日耳曼人，自极地之畔那座庞大的种族摇篮深处倾巢涌出。

===Original===

So again was the growth of the Pictish nation checked and the race hurled into savagery. Erased from the earth, on the fringe of the world with our backs to the wall we fight. Here in Caledon is the last stand of a once mighty race. And we change. Our people have mixed with the savages of an elder age which we drove into the North when we came into the Isles, and now, save for their chieftains, such as thou, Bran, a Pict is strange and abhorrent to look upon.”
“True, true,” said the king impatiently, “but what has that to do﻿—”
“Kull, king of Valusia,” said the wizard imperturbably, “was a barbarian in his age as thou art in thine, though he ruled a mighty empire by the weight of his sword. Gonar, friend of Brule, your first ancestor, has been dead a hundred thousand years as we reckon time. Yet I talked with him a scant hour agone.”
“You talked with his ghost﻿—”
“Or he with mine? Did I go back a hundred thousand years, or did he come forward? If he came to me out of the past, it is not I who talked with a dead man, but he who talked with a man unborn. Past, present and future are one to a wise man. I talked to Gonar while he was alive; likewise was I alive. In a timeless, spaceless land we met and he told me many things.”
The land was growing light with the birth of dawn. The heather waved and bent in long rows before the dawn wind as bowing in worship of the rising sun.
“The jewel in your crown is a magnet that draws down the eons,” said Gonar. “The sun is rising﻿—and who comes out of the sunrise?”
Cormac and the king started. The sun was just lifting a red orb above the eastern hills. And full in the glow, etched boldly against the golden rim, a man suddenly appeared. They had not seen him come. Against the golden birth of day he loomed colossal; a gigantic god from the dawn of creation. Now as he strode toward them the waking hosts saw him and sent up a sudden shout of wonder.
“Who﻿—or what is it?” exclaimed Bran.
“Let us go to meet him, Bran,” answered the wizard. “He is the king Gonar has sent to save the people of Brule.”

===Chinese===

“于是皮克特王国的生机再度横遭阻断，全族重新被轰入蛮荒泥淖之中。在这片被世人所遗忘的蛮荒边陲，我们背水一战。在这片喀里多尼亚的冻土荒原上，矗立着昔日强盛帝国的最后一抹余晖。而我们亦在蜕变。在踏入不列颠群岛之际，我族先民与当年被我们驱赶至北方的史前蛮夷混血通婚；及至如今，除却如你布兰这般大酋长嫡传之外，寻常皮克特人的模样已长得古怪畸形、令人望而生厌。”

“这些尽皆属实，”国王不耐烦地打断道，“但这同明日之战又有何……”

“瓦卢西亚之王库尔，”大巫神色泰然自若、波澜不惊地徐徐道来，“在他那个纪元之中，亦如你今日一般乃是个不折不扣的蛮荒狂徒，纵使他凭借手中重剑的赫赫神威统御着一座浩瀚帝国。布鲁尔的莫逆之交、你的开山始祖戈纳尔，若依凡俗岁月度量，早已归天长眠了十万年。然而就在不足一个时辰之前，老夫尚自同他促膝长谈。”

“你是在同他的亡魂幽灵交谈……”

“亦或是他在同我的幽魂对饮？究竟是老夫逆流溯回了十万年岁月，还是他跨越万古降临于今？倘若他是自洪荒昔日迈向老夫，那便绝非老夫在同死人言说，而是他在同一位尚未出世的后生促膝长谈！对于洞悉玄机的智者而言，过去、现在与未来本为一体。老夫同戈纳尔交谈之际，他尚自生机勃勃；而老夫亦同样神完气足。在超越时空藩篱的极乐净土之中我们神游相会，他向老夫吐露了万千机密。”

晨曦微露，天地万物在破晓的微光中渐渐泛起清明。漫山遍野的石楠花在拂晓长风的吹拂下如波涛般伏拜起伏，宛如在向初升的朝阳顶礼膜拜。

“你铁冠上的红宝石，乃是一座引动万古岁月的磁石枢纽，”戈纳尔幽幽说道，“旭日正在东升——且看是谁踏着破晓的霞光昂然行来？”

科尔马克与年轻国王浑身剧烈一震。一轮猩红的旭日正自东方群峦之后喷薄跃出。而在那万道霞光的正中央，在那轮璀璨的金阳巨轮映衬之下，一道伟岸如天神般的身影赫然凌空迈步而出！他们甚至未曾看清他究竟是如何现身的。沐浴在金色的破晓光芒之中，那道身躯庞大巍峨如山岳巨擘；俨然是从天地初开的太古鸿蒙深处大步踏出的绝世神明！此刻，随着他大步流星跨步而来，苏醒的漫山遍野各族军团亦尽皆目睹了这尊神明，天地间骤然炸响一阵撼天动地的极度骇异狂呼！

“他……他究竟是何方神圣？”布兰失声惊呼。

“随老夫前去迎驾吧，布兰，”大巫泰然自若地朗声答道，“他便是先祖戈纳尔跨越十万年光阴、专为拯救布鲁尔后裔而送来的至尊王者！”

## Rattle of Bones / 白骨咔嗒
===Original===

“Landlord, ho!” The shout broke the lowering silence and reverberated through the black forest with sinister echoing.
“This place hath a forbidding aspect, meseemeth.”
Two men stood in front of the forest tavern. The building was low, long and rambling, built of heavy logs. Its small windows were heavily barred and the door was closed. Above the door its sinister sign showed faintly﻿—a cleft skull.
This door swung slowly open and a bearded face peered out. The owner of the face stepped back and motioned his guests to enter﻿—with a grudging gesture it seemed. A candle gleamed on a table; a flame smoldered in the fireplace.
“Your names?”
“Solomon Kane,” said the taller man briefly.
“Gaston l’Armon,” the other spoke curtly. “But what is that to you?”
“Strangers are few in the Black Forest,” grunted the host, “bandits many. Sit at yonder table and I will bring food.”
The two men sat down, with the bearing of men who have traveled far. One was a tall gaunt man, clad in a featherless hat and somber black garments, which set off the dark pallor of his forbidding face. The other was of a different type entirely, bedecked with lace and plumes, although his finery was somewhat stained from travel. He was handsome in a bold way, and his restless eyes shifted from side to side, never still an instant.
The host brought wine and food to the rough-hewn table and then stood back in the shadows, like a somber image. His features, now receding into vagueness, now luridly etched in the firelight as it leaped and flickered, were masked in a beard which seemed almost animal-like in thickness. A great nose curved above this beard and two small red eyes stared unblinkingly at his guests.
“Who are you?” suddenly asked the younger man.
“I am the host of the Cleft Skull Tavern,” sullenly replied the other. His tone seemed to challenge his questioner to ask further.
“Do you have many guests?” l’Armon pursued.
“Few come twice,” the host grunted.
Kane started and glanced up straight into those small red eyes, as if he sought for some hidden meaning in the host’s words. The flaming eyes seemed to dilate, then dropped sullenly before the Englishman’s cold stare.
“I’m for bed,” said Kane abruptly, bringing his meal to a close. “I must take up my journey by daylight.”
“And I,” added the Frenchman. “Host, show us to our chambers.”

===Chinese===

“掌柜的，开门呐！”这声断喝撕裂了阴沉的死寂，在幽暗黑森林中激起阵阵不祥的回响。

“依我看，这地方透着一股凶险晦气。”

两名男子伫立在森林客栈门前。那是一座由粗重原木筑成的低矮、狭长而杂乱的木屋。小窗上嵌着沉重的铁栅，大门紧闭。在大门上方，隐约悬挂着一块令人毛骨悚然的招牌——一颗被劈裂的颅骨（Cleft Skull）。

房门吱呀一声缓缓向内开启，一张虬髯面孔探了出来。那人后退半步，示意客人进门——动作显得颇为不情不愿。一张方桌上烛火摇曳；壁炉里残火明灭。

“二位尊姓大名？”

“所罗门·凯恩（Solomon Kane），”身材高大的汉子言简意赅。

“加斯东·拉蒙（Gaston l’Armon），”另一人冷冷应道，“不过这与你何干？”

“黑森林里少有生客，”掌柜的粗声嘟囔道，“多的是杀人越货的绿林大盗。且在那张桌旁落座吧，我去取些吃食。”
